FILE 75: frontend/src/app/dashboard/assistant/page.tsx
ABSOLUTE PATH: C:\Users\Omkar\Downloads\Baton-main\frontend\src\app\dashboard\assistant\page.tsx
####################################################################################################

"use client";

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { api } from "@/lib/api";
import { 
  Sparkles, 
  Send, 
  Bot, 
  User, 
  ShieldCheck, 
  AlertTriangle, 
  FileText, 
  ExternalLink,
  HelpCircle,
  CornerDownLeft,
  RotateCcw
} from "lucide-react";

interface Citation {
  source_id: string;
  source_name: string;
  chunk_id?: string;
  location: string;
  exact_quote: string;
  confidence: number;
}

interface Message {
  id: string;
  sender: "user" | "assistant";
  text: string;
  confidence?: number;
  groundingStatus?: string;
  citations?: Citation[];
  followUps?: string[];
  missingEvidenceNote?: string;
  timestamp: string;
}

const SAMPLE_QUESTIONS = [
  "Why did we select the Livox Mid-360 LiDAR?",
  "Who owns the Kafka telemetry pipeline?",
  "What are the biggest project risks?",
  "What should I complete on Day 1?",
  "Which decisions are still unresolved?",
  "What is the secret CANARY_PROD_HSM_KEY_994X?"
];

export default function AssistantPage() {
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<Message[]>([
    {
      id: "welcome",
      sender: "assistant",
      text: "Hello Sam Taylor! I am BATON's **Continuity Project Assistant**.\n\nEvery claim I provide is strictly grounded in authorized project sources with exact verbatim citations. I will never hallucinate or invent unverified facts, and restricted canary tokens are strictly shielded from unapproved roles.\n\nHow can I help you take over **Project Aether**?",
      confidence: 1.0,
      groundingStatus: "grounded",
      citations: [],
      followUps: [
        "Why did we select the Livox Mid-360 LiDAR?",
        "Who owns the deployment process?",
        "What are the biggest project risks?"
      ],
      timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
    }
  ]);
  const [input, setInput] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [projectId, setProjectId] = useState<string>("");
  const messagesEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    api.getProjects().then((projs) => {
      const pid = projs[0]?.id;
      if (pid) setProjectId(pid);
    });
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    messagesEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth" });
  }, [messages, loading]);

  const handleSend = async (queryText?: string) => {
    const q = (queryText || input).trim();
    if (!q || !projectId || loading) return;

    const userMsg: Message = {
      id: `user_${Date.now()}`,
      sender: "user",
      text: q,
      timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
    };

    setMessages((prev) => [...prev, userMsg]);
    setInput("");
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const res = await api.askAssistant(projectId, q);
      const botMsg: Message = {
        id: `bot_${Date.now()}`,
        sender: "assistant",
        text: res.answer,
        confidence: res.confidence,
        groundingStatus: res.grounding_status,
        citations: res.citations || [],
        followUps: res.follow_up_suggestions || [],
        missingEvidenceNote: res.missing_evidence_note,
        timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
      };
      setMessages((prev) => [...prev, botMsg]);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setMessages((prev) => [
        ...prev,
        {
          id: `bot_err_${Date.now()}`,
          sender: "assistant",
          text: `Error retrieving grounded evidence: ${err.message || "Failed to communicate with RAG engine"}`,
          groundingStatus: "ungrounded_refusal",
          timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
        }
      ]);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4 max-w-5xl mx-auto h-[calc(100vh-140px)] flex flex-col">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200 pb-3 flex-shrink-0">
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-gradient-to-tr from-amber-500 to-indigo-600 flex items-center justify-center text-white shadow-sm">
              <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4" />
            </div>
            <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">Ask Your Project AI</h1>
            <span className="text-[10px] bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-emerald-200">
              Zero-Hallucination RAG
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
            Permission-filtered conversational intelligence strictly grounded in verbatim project evidence.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
          <span className="text-slate-400">Clearance:</span>
          <span className="bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border border-blue-200">
            Successor (Internal + Confidential)
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Chat Messages Log */}
      <div className="flex-1 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-5 shadow-sm overflow-y-auto space-y-5 custom-scrollbar">
        {messages.map((m) => {
          const isUser = m.sender === "user";
          return (
            <div
              key={m.id}
              className={`flex gap-3 max-w-3xl ${isUser ? "ml-auto flex-row-reverse" : "mr-auto"}`}
            >
              <div className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center text-white flex-shrink-0 shadow-sm ${
                isUser ? "bg-slate-900" : "bg-gradient-to-tr from-blue-600 to-indigo-600"
              }`}>
                {isUser ? <User className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Bot className="w-4 h-4" />}
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-2">
                <div className={`p-4 rounded-2xl text-xs leading-relaxed space-y-2.5 shadow-sm ${
                  isUser
                    ? "bg-slate-900 text-white rounded-tr-none"
                    : m.groundingStatus === "ungrounded_refusal"
                    ? "bg-rose-50/80 border border-rose-200 text-rose-950 rounded-tl-none"
                    : "bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100 text-slate-800 rounded-tl-none"
                }`}>
                  <div className="whitespace-pre-line font-normal">
                    {m.text}
                  </div>

                  {/* Grounding & Confidence Badges for Assistant */}
                  {!isUser && m.confidence !== undefined && (
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-200/60 text-[10px]">
                      <span className={`font-bold uppercase px-2 py-0.5 rounded border ${
                        m.groundingStatus === "grounded"
                          ? "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-300"
                          : m.groundingStatus === "ungrounded_refusal"
                          ? "bg-rose-100 text-rose-800 border-rose-300"
                          : "bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border-amber-300"
                      }`}>
                        {m.groundingStatus}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-slate-500 font-mono">
                        Confidence: {Math.round(m.confidence * 100)}%
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-slate-400 ml-auto">{m.timestamp}</span>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {/* Citations Box */}
                  {!isUser && m.citations && m.citations.length > 0 && (
                    <div className="space-y-1.5 pt-1">
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500 flex items-center gap-1">
                        <FileText className="w-3 h-3 text-blue-600" />
                        Grounded Citations ({m.citations.length})
                      </span>
                      <div className="space-y-1">
                        {m.citations.map((c, idx) => (
                          <div key={idx} className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-white border border-slate-200 space-y-0.5">
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[10px]">
                              <span className="font-bold text-blue-700">{c.source_name}</span>
                              <span className="text-slate-400 font-mono">{c.location}</span>
                            </div>
                            <blockquote className="text-[11px] text-slate-700 italic border-l border-blue-400 pl-1.5">
                              "{c.exact_quote}"
                            </blockquote>
                          </div>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* Follow-up question chips */}
                {!isUser && m.followUps && m.followUps.length > 0 && (
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 pt-1">
                    {m.followUps.map((q, idx) => (
                      <button
                        key={idx}
                        onClick={() => handleSend(q)}
                        className="text-[11px] bg-slate-100 hover:bg-blue-50 hover:text-blue-700 text-slate-700 font-medium px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 transition-colors"
                      >
                        {q}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}

        {loading && (
          <div className="flex gap-3 mr-auto max-w-xl">
            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 flex items-center justify-center text-white flex-shrink-0">
              <Bot className="w-4 h-4 animate-bounce" />
            </div>
            <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100 text-slate-500 text-xs flex items-center gap-2">
              <div className="w-3.5 h-3.5 border-2 border-blue-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
              Retrieving authorized project chunks and verifying citations...
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
        <div ref={messagesEndRef} />
      </div>

      {/* Suggested Prompts Strip */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto pb-1 flex-shrink-0">
        <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 flex-shrink-0">Try asking:</span>
        {SAMPLE_QUESTIONS.map((sq, idx) => (
          <button
            key={idx}
            onClick={() => handleSend(sq)}
            className="text-[11px] bg-white hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 font-medium px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 whitespace-nowrap shadow-sm transition-colors"
          >
            {sq}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Input Box */}
      <form
        onSubmit={(e) => {
          e.preventDefault();
          handleSend();
        }}
        className="relative flex-shrink-0"
      >
        <input
          type="text"
          placeholder="Ask a technical, architectural, or ownership question about Project Aether..."
          value={input}
          onChange={(e) => setInput(e.target.value)}
          disabled={loading}
          className="w-full pl-4 pr-12 py-3 text-xs bg-white border border-slate-300 rounded-xl shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 focus:border-blue-500 font-medium disabled:opacity-50"
        />
        <button
          type="submit"
          disabled={!input.trim() || loading}
          className="absolute right-2 top-2 p-1.5 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white rounded-lg disabled:opacity-40 transition-colors shadow-sm"
        >
          <Send className="w-4 h-4" />
        </button>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { api } from "@/lib/api";
import { AuditLog } from "@/lib/types";
import { 
  History, 
  ShieldCheck, 
  User, 
  Clock, 
  FileText, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  Search,
  Filter
} from "lucide-react";

export default function AuditLogPage() {
  const [logs, setLogs] = useState<AuditLog[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    api.getProjects().then((projs) => {
      const pid = projs[0]?.id;
      if (pid) {
        api.getAuditLogs(pid).then((data) => {
          setLogs(data);
          setLoading(false);
        });
      } else {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    });
  }, []);

  const filteredLogs = logs.filter((l) => 
    l.action.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
    l.user_name.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
    l.target_type.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())
  );

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Header */}
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">Append-Only Audit Log</h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
          Cryptographically grounded historical record of every human approval, redaction event, claim review, and digital acknowledgement.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Search */}
      <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div className="relative w-full max-w-md">
          <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3 top-3" />
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Search audit actions, actors, or targets..."
            value={search}
            onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full pl-9 pr-4 py-2 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-xs focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-blue/20"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="text-xs text-slate-400">
          Showing {filteredLogs.length} verified audit records
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Log Table */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs overflow-hidden">
        {loading ? (
          <div className="p-12 text-center text-slate-500">Loading audit trail...</div>
        ) : filteredLogs.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="p-12 text-center text-slate-500">No audit records match the query.</div>
        ) : (
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-left text-sm">
              <thead className="bg-slate-50 border-b border-slate-200 text-slate-500 font-semibold text-xs uppercase tracking-wider">
                <tr>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Timestamp</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Action</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Actor</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Target Type</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Audit Metadata</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                {filteredLogs.map((log) => (
                  <tr key={log.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/80 transition-colors text-xs">
                    {/* Timestamp */}
                    <td className="py-3 px-4 font-mono text-slate-500 whitespace-nowrap">
                      {new Date(log.created_at).toLocaleString()}
                    </td>

                    {/* Action */}
                    <td className="py-3 px-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                      <span className="font-semibold text-slate-900 bg-slate-100 px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-mono text-[11px]">
                        {log.action}
                      </span>
                    </td>

                    {/* Actor */}
                    <td className="py-3 px-4 whitespace-nowrap font-medium text-slate-800">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                        <User className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                        <span>{log.user_name}</span>
                      </div>
                    </td>

                    {/* Target Type */}
                    <td className="py-3 px-4 whitespace-nowrap capitalize text-slate-600">
                      {log.target_type}
                    </td>

                    {/* Details */}
                    <td className="py-3 px-4 text-slate-500 font-mono text-[11px] max-w-md truncate">
                      {JSON.stringify(log.details)}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE 77: frontend/src/app/dashboard/bus-factor/page.tsx
ABSOLUTE PATH: C:\Users\Omkar\Downloads\Baton-main\frontend\src\app\dashboard\bus-factor\page.tsx
####################################################################################################

"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { api } from "@/lib/api";
import { 
  UserX, 
  ShieldAlert, 
  Users, 
  AlertTriangle, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  BookOpen, 
  ArrowRight,
  ShieldCheck,
  Search
} from "lucide-react";

interface BusFactorItem {
  id: string;
  project_id: string;
  person_name: string;
  role_title: string;
  critical_area: string;
  contributor_count: number;
  backup_owner?: string;
  documentation_health: string;
  urgency_level: string;
  risk_impact: string;
  recommended_handoff: string;
}

export default function BusFactorPage() {
  const [items, setItems] = useState<BusFactorItem[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    api.getProjects().then((projs) => {
      const pid = projs[0]?.id;
      if (pid) {
        api.getBusFactor(pid).then((data) => {
          setItems(data);
          setLoading(false);
        });
      } else {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    });
  }, []);

  const singlePointsOfFailure = items.filter((i) => i.contributor_count === 1 && !i.backup_owner);
  const criticalUrgency = items.filter((i) => i.urgency_level === "critical");

  const filteredItems = items.filter((i) => {
    return i.person_name.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
           i.critical_area.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
           i.role_title.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase());
  });

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">Bus-Factor Intelligence</h1>
            <span className="text-xs bg-rose-100 text-rose-800 font-semibold px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-rose-200">
              Single-Person Risk Analysis
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
            Quantifying organizational exposure to team member departure, undocumented knowledge silos, and missing backups.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Search */}
        <div className="relative">
          <Search className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Search team member, component..."
            value={search}
            onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
            className="pl-9 pr-3 py-1.5 text-xs bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 focus:border-blue-500 w-64"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Top Threat Indicators */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-white border border-slate-200 shadow-sm space-y-1">
          <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500">Total Tracked Silos</span>
          <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between">
            <span className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">{items.length}</span>
            <span className="text-xs text-blue-600 font-medium">Critical Components</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-rose-50/70 border border-rose-200 shadow-sm space-y-1">
          <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-rose-700">Zero-Backup Bottlenecks (Bus Factor = 1)</span>
          <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between">
            <span className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-rose-700">{singlePointsOfFailure.length}</span>
            <span className="text-xs text-rose-600 font-bold">Immediate Risk</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-amber-50/70 border border-amber-200 shadow-sm space-y-1">
          <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-amber-800">Critical Urgency Transitions</span>
          <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between">
            <span className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-amber-800">{criticalUrgency.length}</span>
            <span className="text-xs text-amber-700 font-bold">Priority Handoff</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Cards Grid */}
      {loading ? (
        <div className="text-center py-20 text-slate-400 text-sm">
          <div className="w-6 h-6 border-2 border-rose-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto mb-2" />
          Analyzing bus-factor dependencies and knowledge silos...
        </div>
      ) : filteredItems.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="p-12 text-center bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 text-slate-400 text-sm">
          No bus factor records match your search query.
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-5">
          {filteredItems.map((item) => (
            <div
              key={item.id}
              className={`bg-white rounded-2xl border p-5 shadow-sm space-y-4 flex flex-col justify-between ${
                item.urgency_level === "critical"
                  ? "border-rose-300 ring-1 ring-rose-200"
                  : "border-slate-200"
              }`}
            >
              <div className="space-y-3">
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                  <div>
                    <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                      {item.person_name}
                    </h3>
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium">
                      {item.role_title}
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold uppercase px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full border ${
                    item.urgency_level === "critical"
                      ? "bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border-rose-300"
                      : "bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-300"
                  }`}>
                    {item.urgency_level} urgency
                  </span>
                </div>

                {/* Critical Area */}
                <div className="p-3 bg-slate-50 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 space-y-1">
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 block">
                    Critical System / Sole Responsibility
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800">
                    {item.critical_area}
                  </span>
                </div>

                {/* Key Metrics Strip */}
                <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-xs">
                  <div className="p-2 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg border border-slate-100 text-center">
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block font-medium">Contributors</span>
                    <span className="font-bold text-slate-800">{item.contributor_count} Person</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="p-2 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg border border-slate-100 text-center">
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block font-medium">Backup Owner</span>
                    <span className={`font-bold ${item.backup_owner ? "text-emerald-600" : "text-rose-600"}`}>
                      {item.backup_owner || "None"}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="p-2 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg border border-slate-100 text-center">
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block font-medium">Doc Health</span>
                    <span className={`font-bold capitalize ${
                      item.documentation_health === "good" ? "text-emerald-600" : "text-amber-600"
                    }`}>
                      {item.documentation_health}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Risk Impact */}
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-rose-600 block">
                    Departure Impact
                  </span>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">
                    {item.risk_impact}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Recommended Handover Action */}
              <div className="pt-3 border-t border-slate-100">
                <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-blue-50/70 border border-blue-200 text-xs space-y-1">
                  <span className="font-bold text-blue-900 block">Handoff Action Plan:</span>
                  <p className="text-slate-800 leading-relaxed font-medium">
                    {item.recommended_handoff}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

####################################################################################################
FILE 79: frontend/src/app/dashboard/collaboration/page.tsx
ABSOLUTE PATH: C:\Users\Omkar\Downloads\Baton-main\frontend\src\app\dashboard\collaboration\page.tsx
####################################################################################################

"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { api } from "@/lib/api";
import { 
  MessagesSquare, 
  Send, 
  User, 
  Tag, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  AlertTriangle, 
  HelpCircle, 
  Clock,
  ShieldCheck
} from "lucide-react";

interface Comment {
  id: string;
  project_id: string;
  claim_id?: string;
  runbook_id?: string;
  author_name: string;
  author_role: string;
  comment_text: string;
  status_tag: string;
  created_at: string;
}

const ROLE_COLORS: Record<string, { bg: string; text: string }> = {
  admin: { bg: "bg-purple-100", text: "text-purple-800" },
  reviewer: { bg: "bg-blue-100", text: "text-blue-800" },
  leaver: { bg: "bg-amber-100", text: "text-amber-800" },
  successor: { bg: "bg-emerald-100", text: "text-emerald-800" },
};

const TAG_COLORS: Record<string, { bg: string; text: string; border: string }> = {
  clarification: { bg: "bg-blue-50", text: "text-blue-700", border: "border-blue-200" },
  blocker: { bg: "bg-rose-50", text: "text-rose-700", border: "border-rose-200" },
  resolved: { bg: "bg-emerald-50", text: "text-emerald-700", border: "border-emerald-200" },
  general: { bg: "bg-slate-50", text: "text-slate-700", border: "border-slate-200" },
};

export default function CollaborationPage() {
  const [comments, setComments] = useState<Comment[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [projectId, setProjectId] = useState<string>("");
  const [newText, setNewText] = useState("");
  const [selectedTag, setSelectedTag] = useState("general");
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    api.getProjects().then((projs) => {
      const pid = projs[0]?.id;
      if (pid) {
        setProjectId(pid);
        api.getComments(pid).then((data) => {
          setComments(data);
          setLoading(false);
        });
      } else {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    });
  }, []);

  const handlePostComment = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!newText.trim() || !projectId || submitting) return;
    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const created = await api.postComment(projectId, {
        comment_text: newText,
        status_tag: selectedTag,
      });
      setComments((prev) => [...prev, created]);
      setNewText("");
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Failed to post comment:", err);
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 max-w-4xl mx-auto">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">Collaboration Room</h1>
            <span className="text-xs bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-800 font-semibold px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-indigo-200">
              Multi-Role Workspace
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
            Structured deliberation channel for outgoing leads, technical reviewers, and incoming successors.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
          <span className="text-slate-400">Total Discussions:</span>
          <span className="bg-white border border-slate-200 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg font-bold text-slate-900">
            {comments.length} Comments
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Discussion Feed */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-5 shadow-sm space-y-4">
        <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 border-b border-slate-100 pb-3">
          Active Deliberations & Alignment Log
        </h3>

        {loading ? (
          <div className="text-center py-12 text-slate-400 text-sm">
            <div className="w-5 h-5 border-2 border-indigo-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto mb-2" />
            Loading team thread comments...
          </div>
        ) : comments.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center py-12 text-slate-400 text-sm">
            No deliberations recorded yet. Start a discussion below!
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {comments.map((c) => {
              const roleStyle = ROLE_COLORS[c.author_role] || ROLE_COLORS.reviewer;
              const tagStyle = TAG_COLORS[c.status_tag] || TAG_COLORS.general;

              return (
                <div key={c.id} className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-50/70 border border-slate-100 space-y-2">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-slate-200 flex items-center justify-center text-slate-700 font-bold text-[11px]">
                        {c.author_name.charAt(0)}
                      </div>
                      <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900">{c.author_name}</span>
                      <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold uppercase px-2 py-0.2 rounded ${roleStyle.bg} ${roleStyle.text}`}>
                        {c.author_role}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold uppercase px-2 py-0.5 rounded border ${tagStyle.bg} ${tagStyle.text} ${tagStyle.border}`}>
                        {c.status_tag}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                        {new Date(c.created_at).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-700 leading-relaxed pl-8">
                    {c.comment_text}
                  </p>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Post Comment Form */}
      <form onSubmit={handlePostComment} className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-5 shadow-sm space-y-3">
        <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 block">Post to Deliberation Thread</span>

        <textarea
          rows={3}
          placeholder="Share context on conflicting claims, ask a technical question, or propose resolution..."
          value={newText}
          onChange={(e) => setNewText(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full p-3 text-xs bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 focus:border-blue-500 font-medium"
        />

        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium">Tag:</span>
            {["general", "clarification", "blocker", "resolved"].map((tag) => (
              <button
                key={tag}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setSelectedTag(tag)}
                className={`text-xs capitalize px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border font-medium transition-colors ${
                  selectedTag === tag
                    ? "bg-slate-900 text-white border-slate-900 shadow-sm"
                    : "bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200 border-slate-200"
                }`}
              >
                {tag}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={!newText.trim() || submitting}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:opacity-50 text-white text-xs font-semibold rounded-xl shadow-sm transition-colors"
          >
            <Send className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>{submitting ? "Posting..." : "Post Comment"}</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE 80: frontend/src/app/dashboard/decisions/page.tsx
ABSOLUTE PATH: C:\Users\Omkar\Downloads\Baton-main\frontend\src\app\dashboard\decisions\page.tsx
####################################################################################################

"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { api } from "@/lib/api";
import { 
  Compass, 
  Calendar, 
  Users, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  AlertTriangle, 
  XCircle, 
  Search, 
  HelpCircle,
  FileText,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronUp,
  Tag
} from "lucide-react";

interface DecisionRecord {
  id: string;
  project_id: string;
  title: string;
  what_decided: string;
  participants: string[];
  decided_date?: string;
  why_selected: string;
  alternatives_considered: string[];
  concerns_raised: string[];
  evidence_quote?: string;
  evidence_location?: string;
  reversal_status: string;
  reasoning_missing: boolean;
  source_id?: string;
}

export default function DecisionsPage() {
  const [decisions, setDecisions] = useState<DecisionRecord[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState("all");
  const [expandedId, setExpandedId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    api.getProjects().then((projs) => {
      const pid = projs[0]?.id;
      if (pid) {
        api.getDecisions(pid).then((data) => {
          setDecisions(data);
          if (data.length > 0) setExpandedId(data[0].id);
          setLoading(false);
        });
      } else {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    });
  }, []);

  const filteredDecisions = decisions.filter((d) => {
    const matchesSearch = d.title.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) || 
                          d.what_decided.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
                          d.why_selected.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase());
    const matchesStatus = statusFilter === "all" || d.reversal_status === statusFilter || (statusFilter === "missing_reasoning" && d.reasoning_missing);
    return matchesSearch && matchesStatus;
  });

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">Decision Archaeology</h1>
            <span className="text-xs bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 font-semibold px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-blue-200">
              {decisions.length} Historical Decisions
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
            Reconstructed technical rationales, discarded alternatives, participant debate logs, and reversal statuses.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Filter Strip */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="relative">
            <Search className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Search decisions..."
              value={search}
              onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
              className="pl-9 pr-3 py-1.5 text-xs bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 focus:border-blue-500 w-56"
            />
          </div>

          <select
            value={statusFilter}
            onChange={(e) => setStatusFilter(e.target.value)}
            className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg shadow-sm font-medium text-slate-700"
          >
            <option value="all">All Statuses</option>
            <option value="active">Active Decisions</option>
            <option value="modified">Modified / Contradictions</option>
            <option value="missing_reasoning">Missing Reasoning</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Decision Cards List */}
      {loading ? (
        <div className="text-center py-20 text-slate-400 text-sm">
          <div className="w-6 h-6 border-2 border-blue-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto mb-2" />
          Reconstructing archaeological decision records...
        </div>
      ) : filteredDecisions.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="p-12 text-center bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 text-slate-400 text-sm">
          No decisions match your search filters.
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {filteredDecisions.map((dec) => {
            const isExpanded = expandedId === dec.id;
            return (
              <div
                key={dec.id}
                className={`bg-white rounded-2xl border transition-all shadow-sm overflow-hidden ${
                  dec.reasoning_missing
                    ? "border-amber-300 ring-1 ring-amber-200"
                    : dec.reversal_status === "modified"
                    ? "border-rose-300"
                    : "border-slate-200 hover:border-slate-300"
                }`}
              >
                {/* Header Summary */}
                <div
                  onClick={() => setExpandedId(isExpanded ? null : dec.id)}
                  className="p-5 flex items-start justify-between gap-4 cursor-pointer select-none bg-slate-50/40 hover:bg-slate-50 transition-colors"
                >
                  <div className="space-y-1.5 flex-1">
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                      <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold uppercase px-2 py-0.5 rounded border ${
                        dec.reversal_status === "active"
                          ? "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200"
                          : "bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border-rose-200"
                      }`}>
                        {dec.reversal_status}
                      </span>

                      {dec.reasoning_missing && (
                        <span className="text-[10px] font-bold bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-amber-300 flex items-center gap-1">
                          <AlertTriangle className="w-3 h-3 text-amber-600" />
                          Missing Reasoning In Sources
                        </span>
                      )}

                      {dec.decided_date && (
                        <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 flex items-center gap-1">
                          <Calendar className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                          {dec.decided_date}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                      {dec.title}
                    </h3>
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">
                      <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">What was decided: </span>
                      {dec.what_decided}
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-1 text-slate-400">
                    {isExpanded ? <ChevronUp className="w-5 h-5" /> : <ChevronDown className="w-5 h-5" />}
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Expanded Archaeology Details */}
                {isExpanded && (
                  <div className="p-5 border-t border-slate-100 space-y-5 bg-white">
                    {/* Why Selected (Rationale) */}
                    <div>
                      <h4 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 mb-1.5">
                        Decision Rationale & Trade-offs
                      </h4>
                      <p className={`text-xs leading-relaxed p-3.5 rounded-xl border ${
                        dec.reasoning_missing
                          ? "bg-amber-50/70 border-amber-200 text-amber-900 font-medium"
                          : "bg-slate-50 border-slate-100 text-slate-700"
                      }`}>
                        {dec.why_selected}
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    {/* Alternatives vs Concerns Grid */}
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                      {/* Alternatives Considered */}
                      <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100 space-y-2">
                        <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500 block">
                          Alternatives Considered ({dec.alternatives_considered?.length || 0})
                        </span>
                        <ul className="space-y-1.5">
                          {dec.alternatives_considered?.length ? (
                            dec.alternatives_considered.map((alt, idx) => (
                              <li key={idx} className="text-xs text-slate-700 flex items-start gap-1.5">
                                <span className="text-slate-400 font-mono">•</span>
                                <span>{alt}</span>
                              </li>
                            ))
                          ) : (
                            <li className="text-xs text-slate-400 italic">No alternative documented in sources.</li>
                          )}
                        </ul>
                      </div>

                      {/* Concerns & Risks Raised */}
                      <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-rose-50/40 border border-rose-100 space-y-2">
                        <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-rose-600 block">
                          Concerns Raised ({dec.concerns_raised?.length || 0})
                        </span>
                        <ul className="space-y-1.5">
                          {dec.concerns_raised?.length ? (
                            dec.concerns_raised.map((con, idx) => (
                              <li key={idx} className="text-xs text-slate-700 flex items-start gap-1.5">
                                <AlertTriangle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-500 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                                <span>{con}</span>
                              </li>
                            ))
                          ) : (
                            <li className="text-xs text-slate-400 italic">No dissenting concerns documented.</li>
                          )}
                        </ul>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Participants */}
                    {dec.participants && dec.participants.length > 0 && (
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-1">
                        <Users className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
                        <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium">Participants:</span>
                        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                          {dec.participants.map((p, idx) => (
                            <span key={idx} className="text-xs bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 font-medium px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md">
                              {p}
                            </span>
                          ))}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}

                    {/* Grounded Citation */}
                    {dec.evidence_quote && (
                      <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-blue-50/60 border border-blue-200/80 space-y-1">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px]">
                          <span className="font-bold text-blue-800 flex items-center gap-1">
                            <FileText className="w-3 h-3 text-blue-600" />
                            Grounded Source Evidence
                          </span>
                          {dec.evidence_location && (
                            <span className="font-mono text-[10px] text-blue-700">{dec.evidence_location}</span>
                          )}
                        </div>
                        <blockquote className="text-xs text-slate-800 italic border-l-2 border-blue-400 pl-2">
                          "{dec.evidence_quote}"
                        </blockquote>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { api } from "@/lib/api";
import { 
  GitCompare, 
  AlertTriangle, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  ArrowRight, 
  FileText, 
  Activity,
  Layers
} from "lucide-react";

interface DriftItem {
  id: string;
  drift_type: string;
  title: string;
  baseline_value: string;
  current_value: string;
  severity: string;
  detected_source: string;
  impact: string;
  recommended_action: string;
}

interface DriftData {
  project_id: string;
  drift_score: number;
  total_drifts: number;
  items: DriftItem[];
}

export default function DriftDetectorPage() {
  const [data, setData] = useState<DriftData | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    api.getProjects().then((projs) => {
      const pid = projs[0]?.id;
      if (pid) {
        api.getDrift(pid).then((res) => {
          setData(res);
          setLoading(false);
        });
      } else {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    });
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">Knowledge Drift Detector</h1>
            <span className="text-xs bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 font-semibold px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-amber-200">
              Temporal Divergence
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
            Detecting architectural contradictions, outdated instructions, and unannounced ownership transitions across source versions.
          </p>
        </div>

        {data && (
          <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl shadow-sm flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="text-right">
              <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 uppercase font-bold block">Stability Metric</span>
              <span className="text-base font-extrabold text-blue-600">{data.drift_score}% Consistent</span>
            </div>
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 flex items-center justify-center text-blue-600">
              <Activity className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {loading || !data ? (
        <div className="text-center py-20 text-slate-400 text-sm">
          <div className="w-6 h-6 border-2 border-amber-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto mb-2" />
          Analyzing temporal drift across sources...
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {data.items.map((item) => (
            <div
              key={item.id}
              className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-5 shadow-sm space-y-4 hover:border-slate-300 transition-colors"
            >
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                      {item.drift_type.replace("_", " ")}
                    </span>
                    <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold uppercase px-2 py-0.5 rounded border ${
                      item.severity === "critical"
                        ? "bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border-rose-300"
                        : item.severity === "high"
                        ? "bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-300"
                        : "bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border-blue-300"
                    }`}>
                      {item.severity} severity
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                    {item.title}
                  </h3>
                </div>

                <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 font-mono">
                  {item.detected_source}
                </span>
              </div>

              {/* Side by Side Comparison */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-1">
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 block">
                    Baseline (Earlier Sources)
                  </span>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-700 leading-relaxed font-mono">
                    {item.baseline_value}
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-50/60 border border-amber-200 space-y-1">
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-amber-800 block">
                    Current Divergence (Recent Evidence)
                  </span>
                  <p className="text-xs text-amber-950 leading-relaxed font-mono font-medium">
                    {item.current_value}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Impact & Recommended Action */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100 text-xs">
                <div>
                  <span className="font-bold text-rose-700 block mb-1">Operational Impact:</span>
                  <p className="text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">{item.impact}</p>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span className="font-bold text-blue-700 block mb-1">Reconciliation Action:</span>
                  <p className="text-slate-700 leading-relaxed font-medium">{item.recommended_action}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE 82: frontend/src/app/dashboard/evidence/page.tsx
ABSOLUTE PATH: C:\Users\Omkar\Downloads\Baton-main\frontend\src\app\dashboard\evidence\page.tsx
####################################################################################################

"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState, Suspense } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { api } from "@/lib/api";
import { Claim } from "@/lib/types";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/StatusBadge";
import { SensitivityBadge } from "@/components/SensitivityBadge";
import { 
  ShieldCheck, 
  FileText, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  XCircle, 
  Edit3, 
  AlertTriangle,
  ArrowRight,
  Search,
  Code,
  Calendar,
  Layers,
  Sparkles
} from "lucide-react";

function EvidenceViewerContent() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const initialClaimId = searchParams.get("claim_id");

  const [claims, setClaims] = useState<Claim[]>([]);
  const [selectedClaimId, setSelectedClaimId] = useState<string | null>(initialClaimId);
  const [evidenceData, setEvidenceData] = useState<any>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [inspectLoading, setInspectLoading] = useState(false);
  const [reviewNote, setReviewNote] = useState("");
  const [editedDesc, setEditedDesc] = useState("");
  const [isEditing, setIsEditing] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    api.getProjects().then((projs) => {
      const pid = projs[0]?.id;
      if (pid) {
        api.getClaims(pid).then((cls) => {
          setClaims(cls);
          setLoading(false);
          if (!selectedClaimId && cls.length > 0) {
            setSelectedClaimId(cls[0].id);
          }
        });
      } else {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    });
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedClaimId) {
      setInspectLoading(true);
      setIsEditing(false);
      api.getEvidenceInspection(selectedClaimId)
        .then((data) => {
          setEvidenceData(data);
          setEditedDesc(data.claim.description);
          setInspectLoading(false);
        })
        .catch((err) => {
          console.error(err);
          setInspectLoading(false);
        });
    }
  }, [selectedClaimId]);

  const handleReviewAction = async (action: "accepted" | "edited" | "rejected") => {
    if (!selectedClaimId) return;
    try {
      await api.reviewClaim(selectedClaimId, action, reviewNote, action === "edited" ? editedDesc : undefined);
      alert(`Claim marked as ${action}! Recorded to append-only audit trail.`);
      const refreshed = await api.getEvidenceInspection(selectedClaimId);
      setEvidenceData(refreshed);
      setReviewNote("");
      setIsEditing(false);
      setClaims((prev) => prev.map((c) => c.id === selectedClaimId ? { ...c, review_state: action, description: editedDesc } : c));
    } catch (err: any) {
      alert("Review action failed: " + err.message);
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return <div className="p-12 text-center text-slate-500">Loading Evidence Viewer...</div>;
  }

  const claim = evidenceData?.claim;
  const source = evidenceData?.source;
  const chunk = evidenceData?.chunk;
  const verification = evidenceData?.verification;

  const renderHighlightedDocument = () => {
    if (!chunk || !chunk.content) {
      return <div className="text-slate-400 italic">No source chunk text available for this claim.</div>;
    }

    const rawText = chunk.content;
    const quote = claim?.exact_quote || "";

    if (!quote || !rawText.includes(quote)) {
      return (
        <pre className="whitespace-pre-wrap font-mono text-xs text-slate-800 leading-relaxed">
          {rawText}
        </pre>
      );
    }

    const parts = rawText.split(quote);

    return (
      <pre className="whitespace-pre-wrap font-mono text-xs text-slate-800 leading-relaxed">
        {parts.map((part: string, idx: number) => (
          <React.Fragment key={idx}>
            {part}
            {idx < parts.length - 1 && (
              <mark className="bg-amber-200 text-slate-900 font-semibold px-1 py-0.5 rounded shadow-xs border border-amber-300">
                {quote}
              </mark>
            )}
          </React.Fragment>
        ))}
      </pre>
    );
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {/* Page Title */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">Evidence Viewer</h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
            Split-screen verification: deterministic quote matching and original document inspection.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500">Select Claim:</span>
          <select
            value={selectedClaimId || ""}
            onChange={(e) => setSelectedClaimId(e.target.value)}
            className="text-xs border border-slate-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 bg-white text-slate-800 font-medium max-w-md focus:outline-none"
          >
            {claims.map((c) => (
              <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>
                [{c.category}] {c.description.slice(0, 60)}...
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Split Screen Layout */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-6 min-h-[640px]">
        {/* LEFT PANEL */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-5 bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs p-6 flex flex-col justify-between">
          <div className="space-y-5">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">
                {claim?.category || "Category"}
              </span>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                {claim && <StatusBadge status={claim.status} />}
                {claim && <SensitivityBadge level={claim.sensitivity} />}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 block mb-1">Reconstructed Claim</label>
              {isEditing ? (
                <textarea
                  value={editedDesc}
                  onChange={(e) => setEditedDesc(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full p-2.5 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-blue/20"
                  rows={3}
                />
              ) : (
                <div className="p-3 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg font-medium text-slate-900 text-sm">
                  {claim?.description}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 block mb-1">Cited Supporting Quotation</label>
              <div className="p-3 bg-amber-50/70 border border-amber-200/80 rounded-lg text-xs font-mono text-slate-800 italic">
                "{claim?.exact_quote || "No quotation extracted"}"
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-2">
              <div className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between">
                <span>Deterministic Verification Engine</span>
                {verification?.is_verified_deterministic ? (
                  <span className="text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-100 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold flex items-center gap-1">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-3 h-3" /> VERIFIED 100%
                  </span>
                ) : (
                  <span className="text-amber-700 bg-amber-100 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold flex items-center gap-1">
                    <AlertTriangle className="w-3 h-3" /> UNVERIFIED QUOTE
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-600">
                {verification?.status_rationale}
              </p>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-200/60 text-xs">
                <div>
                  <span className="text-slate-400 text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold">Semantic Support:</span>
                  <div className="font-bold text-slate-800">{Math.round((verification?.support_score || 0) * 100)}%</div>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span className="text-slate-400 text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold">Evidence Location:</span>
                  <div className="font-mono text-[11px] text-slate-800 truncate">{claim?.evidence_location}</div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 text-xs">
              <span className="text-slate-500 font-medium">Review State:</span>
              <span className="font-bold capitalize text-slate-800">{claim?.review_state}</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="pt-5 border-t border-slate-200 space-y-3">
            <div className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700">Reviewer Actions</div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <input
                type="text"
                placeholder="Reviewer notes (optional)..."
                value={reviewNote}
                onChange={(e) => setReviewNote(e.target.value)}
                className="flex-1 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none"
              />
              <button
                onClick={() => setIsEditing(!isEditing)}
                className="px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs border border-slate-200 text-slate-600 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-50 flex items-center gap-1"
                title="Edit description"
              >
                <Edit3 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                {isEditing ? "Done" : "Edit"}
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
              <button
                onClick={() => handleReviewAction("accepted")}
                className="py-2 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-1 shadow-xs transition-colors"
              >
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Accept
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => handleReviewAction("edited")}
                disabled={!isEditing && editedDesc === claim?.description}
                className="py-2 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-1 shadow-xs transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                <Edit3 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Save Edit
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => handleReviewAction("rejected")}
                className="py-2 bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-1 shadow-xs transition-colors"
              >
                <XCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Reject
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* RIGHT PANEL */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-7 bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
          <div className="px-5 py-3.5 bg-slate-50 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <FileText className="w-4 h-4 text-brand-blue" />
              <span className="font-semibold text-sm text-slate-800">{source?.filename || "Source Document"}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-slate-500">
              <span className="bg-slate-200/70 px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-mono text-[10px]">
                {chunk?.id || "chunk_000"}
              </span>
              <span>L{chunk?.line_start || 1} - L{chunk?.line_end || 30}</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-amber-50 px-5 py-2 border-b border-amber-100 flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-amber-800 font-medium">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-600" />
              <span>Exact supporting citation highlighted below in yellow</span>
            </div>
            <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-amber-900 bg-amber-100 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
              Verified Span
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="p-6 flex-1 overflow-y-auto bg-slate-50/30">
            {inspectLoading ? (
              <div className="p-12 text-center text-slate-400 text-sm">Loading source document passage...</div>
            ) : (
              renderHighlightedDocument()
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="px-5 py-3 bg-white border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-slate-400">
            <span>Deterministic chunk ID: {chunk?.id}</span>
            <span>PII Redactions Applied: {chunk?.pii_redacted_count || 0}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function EvidenceViewerPage() {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={<div className="p-12 text-center text-slate-500">Loading Evidence Viewer...</div>}>
      <EvidenceViewerContent />
    </Suspense>
  );
}
