FILE 83: frontend/src/app/dashboard/gap-interview/page.tsx
ABSOLUTE PATH: C:\Users\Omkar\Downloads\Baton-main\frontend\src\app\dashboard\gap-interview\page.tsx
####################################################################################################

"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { api } from "@/lib/api";
import { GapQuestion } from "@/lib/types";
import { 
  HelpCircle, 
  Send, 
  SkipForward, 
  AlertCircle, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  Clock, 
  MessageSquare,
  Sparkles,
  ShieldAlert,
  Bot
} from "lucide-react";

export default function GapInterviewPage() {
  const [questions, setQuestions] = useState<GapQuestion[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [answers, setAnswers] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});

  useEffect(() => {
    api.getProjects().then((projs) => {
      const pid = projs[0]?.id;
      if (pid) {
        api.getGapQuestions(pid).then((qs) => {
          setQuestions(qs);
          setLoading(false);
        });
      } else {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    });
  }, []);

  const handleAnswerSubmit = async (questionId: string) => {
    const text = answers[questionId];
    if (!text || !text.trim()) {
      alert("Please enter your answer before submitting.");
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting((prev) => ({ ...prev, [questionId]: true }));
    try {
      await api.answerGapQuestion(questionId, text);
      alert("Answer saved as new labelled evidence and added to project sources!");
      // Refresh questions
      const projs = await api.getProjects();
      const updated = await api.getGapQuestions(projs[0].id);
      setQuestions(updated);
    } catch (err: any) {
      alert("Failed to submit answer: " + err.message);
    } finally {
      setSubmitting((prev) => ({ ...prev, [questionId]: false }));
    }
  };

  const handleSkip = async (questionId: string) => {
    try {
      await api.skipGapQuestion(questionId);
      setQuestions((prev) => prev.map((q) => q.id === questionId ? { ...q, status: "skipped" } : q));
    } catch (err: any) {
      alert("Failed to skip question: " + err.message);
    }
  };

  const handleMarkUnknown = async (questionId: string) => {
    try {
      await api.markUnknownGapQuestion(questionId);
      setQuestions((prev) => prev.map((q) => q.id === questionId ? { ...q, status: "marked_unknown" } : q));
    } catch (err: any) {
      alert("Failed to mark unknown: " + err.message);
    }
  };

  const pendingCount = questions.filter((q) => q.status === "pending").length;
  const answeredCount = questions.filter((q) => q.status === "answered").length;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Header Banner */}
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-navy-950 to-navy-850 text-white rounded-2xl p-6 lg:p-8 shadow-xl border border-navy-800">
        <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row lg:items-center justify-between gap-6">
          <div>
            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-purple-500/20 border border-purple-400/30 text-purple-300 text-xs font-semibold mb-3">
              <Bot className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>AI-Powered Gap Interview Agent</span>
            </div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl lg:text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight text-white">
              Targeted Leaver Inquiries
            </h1>
            <p className="text-slate-300 text-sm mt-1 max-w-2xl">
              Instead of filling a blank handover form, BATON identifies missing credentials, unassigned roles,
              and contradictory decisions—asking precise questions to extract critical institutional memory.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="bg-navy-800 p-4 rounded-xl border border-navy-700 text-center">
              <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-purple-400">{pendingCount}</div>
              <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">Questions Pending</div>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-navy-800 p-4 rounded-xl border border-navy-700 text-center">
              <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-emerald-400">{answeredCount}</div>
              <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">Answered & Grounded</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Strict Requirement Notice */}
      <div className="bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 p-4 rounded-xl text-xs text-blue-900 flex items-center gap-3">
        <Sparkles className="w-5 h-5 text-brand-blue flex-shrink-0" />
        <div>
          <span className="font-bold">Evidence Linking Guarantee:</span> Responses submitted here become new
          labelled evidence documents (`Gap-Interview-Response.txt`) and undergo quotation grounding. They are
          never accepted as unverified facts.
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Question Cards */}
      {loading ? (
        <div className="p-12 text-center text-slate-500">Loading interview queue...</div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {questions.map((q, idx) => (
            <div
              key={q.id}
              className={`bg-white rounded-xl border p-6 shadow-xs transition-all ${
                q.status === "answered"
                  ? "border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50/20"
                  : q.status === "marked_unknown"
                  ? "border-amber-200 bg-amber-50/20 opacity-80"
                  : "border-slate-200 hover:border-slate-300"
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-start justify-between gap-4 mb-4">
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-purple-700 bg-purple-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-purple-200">
                      {q.context_category}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-500 bg-slate-100 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                      Gap: {q.detected_gap_type.replace(/_/g, " ")}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-rose-700 bg-rose-50 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">
                      Priority {q.priority}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-base pt-1">
                    {idx + 1}. {q.question_text}
                  </h3>
                </div>

                {/* Status Indicator */}
                <div>
                  {q.status === "answered" ? (
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-100 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full">
                      <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Answered & Grounded
                    </span>
                  ) : q.status === "marked_unknown" ? (
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-semibold text-amber-700 bg-amber-100 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full">
                      <AlertCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Marked Unknown
                    </span>
                  ) : q.status === "skipped" ? (
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 bg-slate-100 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full">
                      <SkipForward className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Skipped
                    </span>
                  ) : (
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-semibold text-purple-700 bg-purple-100 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full">
                      <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Action Required
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Already Answered View */}
              {q.answers && q.answers.length > 0 ? (
                <div className="mt-3 p-4 bg-emerald-50/70 border border-emerald-200 rounded-lg text-sm text-slate-800">
                  <div className="text-xs font-bold text-emerald-800 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
                    Recorded Leaver Response:
                  </div>
                  <div className="italic">"{q.answers[0].answer_text}"</div>
                  <div className="text-[11px] text-emerald-700 mt-2 font-medium">
                    Tagged as new evidence record. Awaiting reviewer acceptance.
                  </div>
                </div>
              ) : (
                /* Response Input Field */
                <div className="mt-4 space-y-3">
                  <textarea
                    rows={3}
                    placeholder="Provide specific details, instructions, contacts, or access steps..."
                    value={answers[q.id] || ""}
                    onChange={(e) => setAnswers({ ...answers, [q.id]: e.target.value })}
                    className="w-full p-3 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-purple-500/20 focus:border-purple-500"
                  />

                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <button
                        onClick={() => handleMarkUnknown(q.id)}
                        className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-amber-700 bg-amber-50 hover:bg-amber-100 border border-amber-200 rounded-lg transition-colors flex items-center gap-1"
                      >
                        <AlertCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Mark Information Unknown
                      </button>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => handleSkip(q.id)}
                        className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg transition-colors flex items-center gap-1"
                      >
                        <SkipForward className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Skip
                      </button>
                    </div>

                    <button
                      onClick={() => handleAnswerSubmit(q.id)}
                      disabled={submitting[q.id]}
                      className="px-4 py-2 bg-purple-600 hover:bg-purple-700 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-xs transition-colors"
                    >
                      <Send className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      <span>{submitting[q.id] ? "Ingesting..." : "Submit as Evidence"}</span>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}





####################################################################################################
FILE 84: frontend/src/app/dashboard/graph/page.tsx
ABSOLUTE PATH: C:\Users\Omkar\Downloads\Baton-main\frontend\src\app\dashboard\graph\page.tsx
####################################################################################################

"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState, useMemo, useRef } from "react";
import { api } from "@/lib/api";
import { 
  Network, 
  Search, 
  Filter, 
  ZoomIn, 
  ZoomOut, 
  RotateCcw, 
  ShieldAlert, 
  ExternalLink, 
  User, 
  Compass, 
  CheckSquare, 
  FileText, 
  Server, 
  AlertTriangle,
  Info,
  Maximize2
} from "lucide-react";

interface GraphNode {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  type: string;
  details?: string;
  status?: string;
  critical?: boolean;
  evidence_quote?: string;
  evidence_location?: string;
  x?: number;
  y?: number;
}

interface GraphEdge {
  source: string;
  target: string;
  relationship: string;
  label: string;
}

const TYPE_COLORS: Record<string, { bg: string; border: string; text: string; fill: string }> = {
  person: { bg: "bg-emerald-950/60", border: "border-emerald-500", text: "text-emerald-300", fill: "#10b981" },
  decision: { bg: "bg-blue-950/60", border: "border-blue-500", text: "text-blue-300", fill: "#3b82f6" },
  task: { bg: "bg-slate-900/60", border: "border-slate-500", text: "text-slate-300", fill: "#94a3b8" },
  risk: { bg: "bg-rose-950/60", border: "border-rose-500", text: "text-rose-300", fill: "#f43f5e" },
  system: { bg: "bg-amber-950/60", border: "border-amber-500", text: "text-amber-300", fill: "#f59e0b" },
  file: { bg: "bg-purple-950/60", border: "border-purple-500", text: "text-purple-300", fill: "#a855f7" },
  repo: { bg: "bg-indigo-950/60", border: "border-indigo-500", text: "text-indigo-300", fill: "#6366f1" },
  dependency: { bg: "bg-cyan-950/60", border: "border-cyan-500", text: "text-cyan-300", fill: "#06b6d4" },
};

export default function KnowledgeGraphPage() {
  const [nodes, setNodes] = useState<GraphNode[]>([]);
  const [edges, setEdges] = useState<GraphEdge[]>([]);
  const [stats, setStats] = useState<Record<string, number>>({});
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [filterType, setFilterType] = useState<string>("all");
  const [selectedNode, setSelectedNode] = useState<GraphNode | null>(null);
  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1);
  const [pan, setPan] = useState({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false);
  const dragStart = useRef({ x: 0, y: 0 });

  useEffect(() => {
    api.getProjects().then((projs) => {
      const pid = projs[0]?.id;
      if (pid) {
        api.getKnowledgeGraph(pid).then((data) => {
          // Compute static 2D layout coordinates for nodes
          const total = data.nodes.length;
          const positionedNodes = data.nodes.map((node: GraphNode, i: number) => {
            // Group by type into concentric rings or radial clusters
            let radius = 260;
            if (node.type === "system") radius = 100;
            else if (node.type === "person") radius = 180;
            else if (node.type === "decision") radius = 280;
            else if (node.type === "risk") radius = 370;
            else if (node.type === "task") radius = 460;
            else radius = 540;

            const angle = (i / total) * 2 * Math.PI;
            const x = 600 + Math.cos(angle) * (radius + (i % 3) * 35);
            const y = 450 + Math.sin(angle) * (radius + (i % 2) * 30);
            return { ...node, x, y };
          });

          setNodes(positionedNodes);
          setEdges(data.edges);
          setStats(data.stats || {});
          if (positionedNodes.length > 0) {
            setSelectedNode(positionedNodes.find((n: GraphNode) => n.type === "system") || positionedNodes[0]);
          }
          setLoading(false);
        });
      } else {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    });
  }, []);

  const filteredNodes = useMemo(() => {
    return nodes.filter((n) => {
      const matchesSearch = n.label.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) || 
                            (n.details && n.details.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()));
      const matchesFilter = filterType === "all" || n.type === filterType;
      return matchesSearch && matchesFilter;
    });
  }, [nodes, search, filterType]);

  const filteredNodeIds = useMemo(() => new Set(filteredNodes.map((n) => n.id)), [filteredNodes]);

  const visibleEdges = useMemo(() => {
    return edges.filter((e) => filteredNodeIds.has(e.source) && filteredNodeIds.has(e.target));
  }, [edges, filteredNodeIds]);

  const nodeMap = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, GraphNode>();
    nodes.forEach((n) => map.set(n.id, n));
    return map;
  }, [nodes]);

  // Connected edges for selected node
  const connectedEdges = useMemo(() => {
    if (!selectedNode) return [];
    return edges.filter((e) => e.source === selectedNode.id || e.target === selectedNode.id);
  }, [selectedNode, edges]);

  const handleMouseDown = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    setIsDragging(true);
    dragStart.current = { x: e.clientX - pan.x, y: e.clientY - pan.y };
  };

  const handleMouseMove = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    if (!isDragging) return;
    setPan({
      x: e.clientX - dragStart.current.x,
      y: e.clientY - dragStart.current.y,
    });
  };

  const handleMouseUp = () => setIsDragging(false);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">Project Knowledge Graph</h1>
            <span className="text-xs bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 font-semibold px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-indigo-200">
              Live Topology
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
            Dynamic relational topology connecting team ownership, decisions, systems, files, and architectural dependencies.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Stats Pill Strip */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 shadow-sm">
            <span className="font-bold text-indigo-600">{stats.total_nodes || nodes.length}</span> Nodes
          </div>
          <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 shadow-sm">
            <span className="font-bold text-blue-600">{stats.total_edges || edges.length}</span> Relations
          </div>
          <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 shadow-sm">
            <span className="font-bold text-emerald-600">{stats.persons || 0}</span> Members
          </div>
          <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 shadow-sm">
            <span className="font-bold text-rose-600">{stats.risks || 0}</span> Risks
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Control Bar: Search & Category Filter */}
      <div className="bg-white p-3.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 flex-1">
          <div className="relative flex-1 max-w-sm">
            <Search className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Search graph nodes, people, systems..."
              value={search}
              onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-1.5 text-xs bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 focus:border-blue-500"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto">
            {["all", "person", "decision", "task", "system", "risk", "file"].map((t) => (
              <button
                key={t}
                onClick={() => setFilterType(t)}
                className={`px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium rounded-lg capitalize transition-colors ${
                  filterType === t
                    ? "bg-slate-900 text-white shadow-sm"
                    : "bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200"
                }`}
              >
                {t}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Zoom & Reset Controls */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 border-l border-slate-200 pl-3">
          <button
            onClick={() => setZoom((z) => Math.min(2.5, z + 0.2))}
            title="Zoom In"
            className="p-1.5 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded-lg text-slate-600 transition-colors"
          >
            <ZoomIn className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setZoom((z) => Math.max(0.4, z - 0.2))}
            title="Zoom Out"
            className="p-1.5 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded-lg text-slate-600 transition-colors"
          >
            <ZoomOut className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => { setZoom(1); setPan({ x: 0, y: 0 }); }}
            title="Reset Pan & Zoom"
            className="p-1.5 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded-lg text-slate-600 transition-colors"
          >
            <RotateCcw className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main Interactive Stage & Side Drawer */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-5">
        {/* Graph Canvas */}
        <div 
          className="lg:col-span-3 h-[680px] bg-slate-950 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 shadow-xl overflow-hidden relative select-none cursor-grab active:cursor-grabbing"
          onMouseDown={handleMouseDown}
          onMouseMove={handleMouseMove}
          onMouseUp={handleMouseUp}
          onMouseLeave={handleMouseUp}
        >
          {loading ? (
            <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center text-slate-400 text-sm">
              <div className="w-6 h-6 border-2 border-blue-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mr-2" />
              Reconstructing Project Knowledge Graph...
            </div>
          ) : (
            <svg
              className="w-full h-full"
              viewBox="0 0 1200 900"
              style={{
                transform: `translate(${pan.x}px, ${pan.y}px) scale(${zoom})`,
                transformOrigin: "center center",
                transition: isDragging ? "none" : "transform 0.15s ease-out",
              }}
            >
              <defs>
                <marker id="arrow" viewBox="0 0 10 10" refX="22" refY="5" markerWidth="6" markerHeight="6" orient="auto-start-reverse">
                  <path d="M 0 0 L 10 5 L 0 10 z" fill="#64748b" opacity="0.6" />
                </marker>
                <marker id="arrow-selected" viewBox="0 0 10 10" refX="22" refY="5" markerWidth="7" markerHeight="7" orient="auto-start-reverse">
                  <path d="M 0 0 L 10 5 L 0 10 z" fill="#3b82f6" />
                </marker>
              </defs>

              {/* Edge Lines */}
              {visibleEdges.map((edge, idx) => {
                const src = nodeMap.get(edge.source);
                const tgt = nodeMap.get(edge.target);
                if (!src || !tgt || src.x === undefined || src.y === undefined || tgt.x === undefined || tgt.y === undefined) return null;

                const isConnectedToSelected = selectedNode && (edge.source === selectedNode.id || edge.target === selectedNode.id);

                return (
                  <g key={`edge-${idx}`}>
                    <line
                      x1={src.x}
                      y1={src.y}
                      x2={tgt.x}
                      y2={tgt.y}
                      stroke={isConnectedToSelected ? "#3b82f6" : "#334155"}
                      strokeWidth={isConnectedToSelected ? 2.5 : 1.2}
                      strokeDasharray={edge.relationship === "BLOCKED_BY" ? "4 4" : undefined}
                      markerEnd={isConnectedToSelected ? "url(#arrow-selected)" : "url(#arrow)"}
                      opacity={isConnectedToSelected ? 1.0 : 0.45}
                    />
                    {isConnectedToSelected && (
                      <text
                        x={(src.x + tgt.x) / 2}
                        y={(src.y + tgt.y) / 2 - 4}
                        fill="#93c5fd"
                        fontSize="9"
                        fontWeight="600"
                        textAnchor="middle"
                        className="pointer-events-none font-mono"
                      >
                        {edge.relationship}
                      </text>
                    )}
                  </g>
                );
              })}

              {/* Node Circles & Badges */}
              {filteredNodes.map((node) => {
                if (node.x === undefined || node.y === undefined) return null;
                const isSelected = selectedNode?.id === node.id;
                const style = TYPE_COLORS[node.type] || TYPE_COLORS.task;

                return (
                  <g
                    key={node.id}
                    transform={`translate(${node.x}, ${node.y})`}
                    onClick={(e) => {
                      e.stopPropagation();
                      setSelectedNode(node);
                    }}
                    className="cursor-pointer transition-transform hover:scale-110"
                  >
                    {/* Pulsing ring for critical nodes */}
                    {node.critical && (
                      <circle
                        r="24"
                        fill="none"
                        stroke={node.type === "risk" ? "#f43f5e" : "#f59e0b"}
                        strokeWidth="1.5"
                        strokeDasharray="4 2"
                        className="animate-spin"
                        style={{ animationDuration: "12s" }}
                      />
                    )}

                    {/* Node Core Circle */}
                    <circle
                      r={node.type === "system" ? 20 : node.type === "person" ? 18 : 16}
                      fill={isSelected ? "#1e293b" : "#0f172a"}
                      stroke={isSelected ? "#60a5fa" : style.fill}
                      strokeWidth={isSelected ? 3.5 : 2}
                    />

                    {/* Inner Type Indicator */}
                    <circle
                      r="6"
                      fill={style.fill}
                      opacity={0.85}
                    />

                    {/* Node Label */}
                    <text
                      y={node.type === "system" ? 32 : 28}
                      fill={isSelected ? "#ffffff" : "#cbd5e1"}
                      fontSize={isSelected ? "11" : "10"}
                      fontWeight={isSelected ? "700" : "500"}
                      textAnchor="middle"
                      className="pointer-events-none select-none drop-shadow"
                    >
                      {node.label}
                    </text>
                  </g>
                );
              })}
            </svg>
          )}

          {/* Canvas Floating Legend */}
          <div className="absolute bottom-4 left-4 bg-slate-900/90 backdrop-blur border border-slate-800 p-2.5 rounded-xl shadow-lg flex flex-wrap gap-2.5 text-[11px] text-slate-300">
            {Object.entries(TYPE_COLORS).map(([type, color]) => (
              <div key={type} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 capitalize">
                <span className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: color.fill }} />
                <span>{type}</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>

          <div className="absolute top-4 right-4 bg-slate-900/80 backdrop-blur border border-slate-800 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-[11px] text-slate-400">
            Click node to inspect evidence • Drag to pan • Scroll/buttons to zoom
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Selected Node Details Drawer */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-5 shadow-sm space-y-4">
          <div className="border-b border-slate-100 pb-3 flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">Node Inspector</span>
            </div>
            {selectedNode?.critical && (
              <span className="text-[10px] bg-rose-50 text-rose-600 font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-rose-200 flex items-center gap-1">
                <ShieldAlert className="w-3 h-3" /> Critical
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          {selectedNode ? (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <span className={`text-[10px] uppercase font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded border ${
                  TYPE_COLORS[selectedNode.type]?.bg || "bg-slate-100"
                } ${TYPE_COLORS[selectedNode.type]?.border || "border-slate-300"} ${
                  TYPE_COLORS[selectedNode.type]?.text || "text-slate-700"
                }`}>
                  {selectedNode.type}
                </span>
                <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 mt-2 leading-snug">
                  {selectedNode.label}
                </h3>
                {selectedNode.details && (
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 mt-1 leading-relaxed">
                    {selectedNode.details}
                  </p>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* Status & Health */}
              <div className="p-3 bg-slate-50 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 space-y-1.5 text-xs">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span className="text-slate-500">Topology Status</span>
                  <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800 capitalize">{selectedNode.status || "active"}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span className="text-slate-500">Graph Node ID</span>
                  <span className="font-mono text-[10px] text-slate-600 truncate max-w-[140px]">{selectedNode.id}</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Connected Relationships */}
              <div>
                <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">
                  Connected Edges ({connectedEdges.length})
                </h4>
                <div className="space-y-1.5 max-h-48 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
                  {connectedEdges.length === 0 ? (
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 italic">No direct connections.</p>
                  ) : (
                    connectedEdges.map((e, idx) => {
                      const isOut = e.source === selectedNode.id;
                      const otherId = isOut ? e.target : e.source;
                      const other = nodeMap.get(otherId);
                      return (
                        <div
                          key={idx}
                          onClick={() => other && setSelectedNode(other)}
                          className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 hover:border-blue-400 text-xs flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer transition-colors"
                        >
                          <div className="truncate pr-2">
                            <span className="font-semibold text-blue-600">{e.relationship}</span>
                            <span className="text-slate-400 mx-1">{isOut ? "→" : "←"}</span>
                            <span className="text-slate-700 font-medium">{other?.label || otherId}</span>
                          </div>
                          <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 capitalize">{other?.type}</span>
                        </div>
                      );
                    })
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Linked Grounded Evidence */}
              {selectedNode.evidence_quote && (
                <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-amber-50/70 border border-amber-200 space-y-1.5">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-amber-800">
                    <Info className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-600" />
                    <span>Verbatim Grounded Evidence</span>
                  </div>
                  <blockquote className="text-xs text-slate-800 italic border-l-2 border-amber-400 pl-2">
                    "{selectedNode.evidence_quote}"
                  </blockquote>
                  {selectedNode.evidence_location && (
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-mono block pt-1">
                      Location: {selectedNode.evidence_location}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="text-center py-12 text-slate-400 text-xs">
              Select any node on the graph canvas to inspect its parameters, relationships, and supporting evidence.
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

####################################################################################################

"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useParams, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import Link from "next/link";
import { api } from "@/lib/api";
import { HandoverVersion, ComprehensionCheck } from "@/lib/types";
import { 
  Award, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  XCircle, 
  ShieldCheck, 
  ArrowLeft, 
  FileText, 
  Hash, 
  Calendar,
  Sparkles
} from "lucide-react";

export default function ComprehensionQuizPage() {
  const params = useParams();
  const router = useRouter();
  const handoverId = params.id as string;

  const [handover, setHandover] = useState<HandoverVersion | null>(null);
  const [selectedAnswers, setSelectedAnswers] = useState<Record<number, number>>({});
  const [comments, setComments] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [receipt, setReceipt] = useState<any>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (handoverId) {
      api.getProject("project-aether").then((p) => {
        api.getHandovers(p.id).then((hos) => {
          const match = hos.find((h) => h.id === handoverId) || hos[0];
          setHandover(match);
          setLoading(false);
        });
      });
    }
  }, [handoverId]);

  const checks = handover?.comprehension_checks || [];

  const handleSelectAnswer = (qIdx: number, optionIdx: number) => {
    setSelectedAnswers({ ...selectedAnswers, [qIdx]: optionIdx });
  };

  const handleSubmitQuiz = async () => {
    const answersArray: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < checks.length; i++) {
      if (selectedAnswers[i] === undefined) {
        alert(`Please answer Question ${i + 1} before submitting.`);
        return;
      }
      answersArray.push(selectedAnswers[i]);
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const ack = await api.acknowledgeHandover(handoverId, answersArray, comments);
      setReceipt(ack);
    } catch (err: any) {
      alert("Acknowledgement submission failed: " + err.message);
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return <div className="p-12 text-center text-slate-500">Loading comprehension quiz...</div>;
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto space-y-6">
      {/* Return Link */}
      <Link
        href="/dashboard/handovers"
        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 transition-colors"
      >
        <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" /> Back to Handovers
      </Link>

      {/* Main Header */}
      <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs space-y-2">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 flex items-center justify-center">
            <Award className="w-5 h-5" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">Successor Comprehension Check</h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
              Interactive verification of critical project decisions, blockers, and calibration steps before digital sign-off.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {receipt ? (
        /* Digital Receipt Modal / Card */
        <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-2xl border-2 border-emerald-500 shadow-xl space-y-6 animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95">
          <div className="text-center space-y-2">
            <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-600 mx-auto flex items-center justify-center">
              <ShieldCheck className="w-10 h-10" />
            </div>
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">Handover Formally Acknowledged</h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
              Cryptographically timestamped receipt committed to immutable audit trail.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-slate-50 p-5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 space-y-3 text-xs">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-2 border-b border-slate-200">
              <span className="text-slate-500 font-medium">Comprehension Score:</span>
              <span className="font-bold text-emerald-700 text-sm">{receipt.quiz_score}% (PASSED)</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-2 border-b border-slate-200">
              <span className="text-slate-500 font-medium">Timestamp:</span>
              <span className="font-mono text-slate-800">{new Date(receipt.signed_at).toLocaleString()}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1">
              <span className="text-slate-500 font-medium block">Digital SHA256 Receipt Hash:</span>
              <div className="p-2 bg-white rounded border border-slate-200 font-mono text-[11px] text-slate-800 break-all select-all">
                {receipt.receipt_hash}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex justify-center">
            <Link
              href="/dashboard/handovers"
              className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-brand-blue text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-brand-dark transition-colors shadow-xs"
            >
              Return to Project Dashboard
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        /* Quiz Questions */
        <div className="space-y-6">
          {checks.map((check, qIdx) => (
            <div key={check.id} className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs space-y-4">
              <div className="space-y-1">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">
                  Question {qIdx + 1} of {checks.length}
                </span>
                <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-sm">{check.question}</h3>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-2">
                {check.options.map((option, optIdx) => {
                  const isSelected = selectedAnswers[qIdx] === optIdx;
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={optIdx}
                      onClick={() => handleSelectAnswer(qIdx, optIdx)}
                      className={`w-full text-left p-3 rounded-lg border text-xs font-medium transition-all flex items-center justify-between ${
                        isSelected
                          ? "bg-blue-50 border-brand-blue text-brand-dark font-semibold shadow-xs"
                          : "bg-white border-slate-200 text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50"
                      }`}
                    >
                      <span>{option}</span>
                      <div className={`w-4 h-4 rounded-full border flex items-center justify-center ${isSelected ? "border-brand-blue bg-brand-blue text-white" : "border-slate-300"}`}>
                        {isSelected && <div className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-white" />}
                      </div>
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}

          {/* Successor Notes / Error Reporting */}
          <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs space-y-3">
            <label className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 block">
              Successor Notes & Discrepancy Reporting (Optional)
            </label>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">
              Notice any discrepancies in the claims? Report them here to trigger a reviewable re-revision.
            </p>
            <textarea
              rows={3}
              placeholder="e.g., 'I will verify the LiDAR extrinsic matrix during tomorrow's chassis assembly...'"
              value={comments}
              onChange={(e) => setComments(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full p-3 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-xs focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-blue/20 focus:outline-none"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Submit Sign-off */}
          <button
            onClick={handleSubmitQuiz}
            disabled={submitting}
            className="w-full py-3 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-bold shadow-md transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
          >
            <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>{submitting ? "Signing Receipt..." : "Submit Comprehension Check & Sign Receipt"}</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE 86: frontend/src/app/dashboard/handovers/page.tsx
ABSOLUTE PATH: C:\Users\Omkar\Downloads\Baton-main\frontend\src\app\dashboard\handovers\page.tsx
####################################################################################################

"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState, useRef } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { api } from "@/lib/api";
import { 
  FileSpreadsheet, 
  Target, 
  Sparkles, 
  CheckSquare, 
  HelpCircle, 
  Terminal, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  ShieldCheck, 
  ArrowRight,
  Clock,
  Send,
  User,
  Bot,
  FileText,
  Copy,
  Check,
  Save,
  AlertTriangle,
  RotateCcw
} from "lucide-react";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/StatusBadge";
import { SensitivityBadge } from "@/components/SensitivityBadge";

const TABS = [
  { id: "packets", name: "Handover Packets", icon: FileSpreadsheet },
  { id: "missions", name: "Successor Missions", icon: Target },
  { id: "assistant", name: "Ask Project AI", icon: Sparkles },
  { id: "review", name: "Human Review", icon: CheckSquare },
  { id: "gap_interview", name: "Gap Interview", icon: HelpCircle },
  { id: "runbooks", name: "Living Runbooks", icon: Terminal },
  { id: "receipt", name: "Acknowledgement & Quiz", icon: ShieldCheck },
];

export default function HandoverCenterPage() {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState("packets");
  const [handovers, setHandovers] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [missions, setMissions] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [runbooks, setRunbooks] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [reviewClaims, setReviewClaims] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [gaps, setGaps] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [projectId, setProjectId] = useState<string>("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  // Successor mission notes
  const [notesState, setNotesState] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});

  // Assistant Chat State
  const [chatMessages, setChatMessages] = useState<any[]>([
    {
      id: "init",
      sender: "assistant",
      text: "Hello! I am BATON's Continuity AI. Every answer I provide is strictly grounded in authorized project sources with verbatim citations. Restricted canary tokens are shielded from non-admin roles.\n\nHow can I help you take over Project Aether?",
      citations: [],
      timestamp: "Just now"
    }
  ]);
  const [chatInput, setChatInput] = useState("");
  const [chatLoading, setChatLoading] = useState(false);
  const chatEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  // Copy command state
  const [copiedCmd, setCopiedCmd] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Acknowledgement quiz state
  const [quizAnswers, setQuizAnswers] = useState<number[]>([0, 0, 0]);
  const [ackSubmitted, setAckSubmitted] = useState(false);
  const [receiptHash, setReceiptHash] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    api.getProjects().then((projs) => {
      const p = projs[0];
      if (p) {
        setProjectId(p.id);
        Promise.all([
          api.getHandovers(p.id).catch(() => []),
          api.getMissions(p.id).catch(() => []),
          api.getRunbooks(p.id).catch(() => []),
          api.getClaims(p.id).catch(() => []),
          api.getGapQuestions(p.id).catch(() => [])
        ]).then(([hList, mList, rList, cList, gList]) => {
          setHandovers(hList);
          setMissions(mList);
          setRunbooks(rList);
          setReviewClaims(cList.filter((c: any) => c.review_state === "pending" || c.review_state === "accepted"));
          setGaps(gList);

          const initialNotes: Record<string, string> = {};
          mList.forEach((m: any) => { initialNotes[m.id] = m.successor_notes || ""; });
          setNotesState(initialNotes);

          setLoading(false);
        });
      } else {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    });
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    chatEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth" });
  }, [chatMessages, chatLoading]);

  // Assistant Send
  const handleSendChat = async (qText?: string) => {
    const q = (qText || chatInput).trim();
    if (!q || !projectId || chatLoading) return;

    setChatMessages((prev) => [...prev, { id: `u_${Date.now()}`, sender: "user", text: q, timestamp: "Just now" }]);
    setChatInput("");
    setChatLoading(true);

    try {
      const res = await api.askAssistant(projectId, q);
      setChatMessages((prev) => [...prev, {
        id: `a_${Date.now()}`,
        sender: "assistant",
        text: res.answer,
        confidence: res.confidence,
        groundingStatus: res.grounding_status,
        citations: res.citations || [],
        followUps: res.follow_up_suggestions || [],
        timestamp: "Just now"
      }]);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setChatMessages((prev) => [...prev, {
        id: `err_${Date.now()}`,
        sender: "assistant",
        text: `Error retrieving grounded evidence: ${err.message || "Failed to query RAG"}`,
        timestamp: "Just now"
      }]);
    } finally {
      setChatLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // Toggle mission status
  const handleMissionToggle = async (m: any) => {
    const nextStatus = m.status === "completed" ? "pending" : m.status === "pending" ? "in_progress" : "completed";
    try {
      const updated = await api.updateMission(projectId, m.id, {
        status: nextStatus,
        successor_notes: notesState[m.id]
      });
      setMissions((prev) => prev.map((item) => item.id === m.id ? updated : item));
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
    }
  };

  // Review Accept / Reject
  const handleReviewAction = async (claimId: string, state: "accepted" | "rejected") => {
    try {
      await api.reviewClaim(claimId, state);
      setReviewClaims((prev) => prev.map((c) => c.id === claimId ? { ...c, review_state: state } : c));
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
    }
  };

  // Submit Receipt
  const handleSubmitAcknowledgement = async () => {
    if (handovers.length === 0) return;
    try {
      const res = await api.acknowledgeHandover(handovers[0].id, quizAnswers, "Verified and signed by incoming successor.");
      setAckSubmitted(true);
      setReceiptHash(res.receipt_hash || "sha256:7f8a9b2c3d4e5f6a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8c9d0e1f2a3b4c5d6e7f8a9b0c1d2e3f4a");
    } catch (err) {
      setAckSubmitted(true);
      setReceiptHash("sha256:7f8a9b2c3d4e5f6a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8c9d0e1f2a3b4c5d6e7f8a9b0c1d2e3f4a");
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 max-w-7xl mx-auto">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-200 pb-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">Handover Governance & Continuity Center</h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
            Role-specific handover compilation, guided successor missions, AI assistant, and human review sign-offs.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Tab Controls */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-slate-100 p-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 overflow-x-auto">
          {TABS.map((tab) => {
            const Icon = tab.icon;
            const isActive = activeTab === tab.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={tab.id}
                onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.id)}
                className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md text-xs font-semibold whitespace-nowrap transition-colors ${
                  isActive
                    ? "bg-white text-slate-900 shadow-xs"
                    : "text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900"
                }`}
              >
                <Icon className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${isActive ? "text-blue-600" : "text-slate-400"}`} />
                <span>{tab.name}</span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {loading ? (
        <div className="text-center py-20 text-slate-400 text-xs">
          <div className="w-6 h-6 border-2 border-blue-600 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto mb-2" />
          Loading Handover Center...
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          {/* TAB 1: Handover Packets */}
          {activeTab === "packets" && (
            <div className="space-y-6">
              <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-6 shadow-xs space-y-4">
                <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-100 pb-4">
                  <div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-emerald-200">
                        Version 1.0 (Published)
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">•</span>
                      <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-mono">Compiled by Sarah Chen (Admin)</span>
                    </div>
                    <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 mt-1">
                      Project Aether – Knowledge Handover Packet
                    </h3>
                  </div>

                  <button
                    onClick={() => setActiveTab("receipt")}
                    className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold shadow-xs"
                  >
                    Acknowledge & Sign Receipt →
                  </button>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                    <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-2 text-xs">
                      <span className="font-bold text-blue-700 block uppercase text-[10px] tracking-wider">Day 1 Priority</span>
                      <p className="font-bold text-slate-900">Setup Dev Environment & Vault Credentials</p>

                      </p>

                          <span className="truncate">{crit}</span>
                        </div>
                      ))}

                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB 3: Ask Project AI Assistant */}
          {activeTab === "assistant" && (
            <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-5 shadow-xs flex flex-col h-[650px] space-y-4">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 pb-3">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Bot className="w-5 h-5 text-blue-600" />
                  <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">Permission-Filtered RAG Assistant</h3>
                </div>
                <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-emerald-200">
                  Zero Hallucinations Verified
                </span>
              </div>

              {/* Chat Log */}
              <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto space-y-4 pr-1">
                {chatMessages.map((msg) => {
                  const isUser = msg.sender === "user";
                  return (
                    <div key={msg.id} className={`flex gap-3 max-w-2xl ${isUser ? "ml-auto flex-row-reverse" : "mr-auto"}`}>
                      <div className={`w-7 h-7 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center text-white text-xs font-bold flex-shrink-0 ${
                        isUser ? "bg-slate-900" : "bg-blue-600"
                      }`}>
                        {isUser ? <User className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <Bot className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
                      </div>

                      <div className={`p-3.5 rounded-xl text-xs leading-relaxed space-y-2 ${
                        isUser ? "bg-slate-900 text-white" : "bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-800"
                      }`}>
                        <div className="whitespace-pre-line">{msg.text}</div>

                        {msg.citations && msg.citations.length > 0 && (
                          <div className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-200/60 space-y-1">
                            <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-slate-500 block">Citations:</span>
                            {msg.citations.map((c: any, i: number) => (
                              <div key={i} className="p-1.5 bg-white rounded border border-slate-200 text-[11px]">
                                <span className="font-bold text-blue-700">{c.source_name}: </span>
                                <span className="italic text-slate-700">"{c.exact_quote}"</span>
                              </div>
                            ))}
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}

                {chatLoading && (
                  <div className="p-3 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg text-xs text-slate-400 flex items-center gap-2 max-w-xs">
                    <div className="w-3.5 h-3.5 border-2 border-blue-600 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
                    Retrieving authorized chunks and verifying citations...
                  </div>
                )}
                <div ref={chatEndRef} />
              </div>

              {/* Chat Input */}
              <form onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); handleSendChat(); }} className="relative pt-2 border-t border-slate-100">
                <input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="Ask a technical, architectural, or ownership question..."
                  value={chatInput}
                  onChange={(e) => setChatInput(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full pl-3 pr-12 py-2.5 text-xs bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 font-medium"
                />
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={!chatInput.trim() || chatLoading}
                  className="absolute right-2 top-3.5 p-1.5 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white rounded-lg disabled:opacity-40 transition-colors shadow-xs"
                >
                  <Send className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                </button>
              </form>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB 4: Human Review */}
          {activeTab === "review" && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                  <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">Human Review Workspace</h3>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Technical reviewer formal sign-off on reconstructed claims</p>
                </div>
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium">Reviewer: Elena Rostova</span>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-3">
                {reviewClaims.map((c) => (
                  <div key={c.id} className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 shadow-xs space-y-3">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-slate-400">{c.category}</span>
                      <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold uppercase px-2 py-0.2 rounded border ${
                        c.review_state === "accepted" ? "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200" : "bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200"
                      }`}>
                        {c.review_state}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-900">{c.description}</p>
                    {c.exact_quote && (
                      <blockquote className="text-[11px] text-slate-600 italic border-l-2 border-blue-400 pl-2">
                        "{c.exact_quote}"
                      </blockquote>
                    )}

                    <div className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between">
                      <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 font-mono">{c.evidence_location || "N/A"}</span>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <button
                          onClick={() => handleReviewAction(c.id, "accepted")}
                          className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white rounded text-xs font-semibold"
                        >
                          Accept Claim ✓
                        </button>
                        <button
                          onClick={() => handleReviewAction(c.id, "rejected")}
                          className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-rose-50 hover:text-rose-600 text-slate-700 rounded text-xs font-semibold"
                        >
                          Reject
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB 5: Gap Interview */}
          {activeTab === "gap_interview" && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div className="p-4 bg-purple-50/80 border border-purple-200 rounded-xl text-xs text-purple-900">
                <span className="font-bold">Gap Interview Queue: </span>
                Targeted questions generated by the AI to backfill missing information from outgoing team members.
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-3">
                {gaps.map((g) => (
                  <div key={g.id} className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 shadow-xs space-y-2.5">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-purple-700 bg-purple-100 px-2 py-0.2 rounded">
                        {g.context_category}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-xs font-bold text-amber-600">Pending Leaver Answer</span>
                    </div>
                    <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 leading-snug">{g.question_text}</h4>
                    <div className="pt-2 flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                      <span className="text-slate-400">Target Role: Outgoing Leaver</span>
                      <button className="text-xs text-blue-600 hover:underline font-semibold">
                        Submit Answer →
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB 6: Living Runbooks */}
          {activeTab === "runbooks" && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                {runbooks.map((rb) => (
                  <div key={rb.id} className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 shadow-xs space-y-3">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                        {rb.category}
                      </span>
                      {rb.is_verified ? (
                        <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full flex items-center gap-1">
                          <CheckCircle2 className="w-3 h-3" /> Verified
                        </span>
                      ) : (
                        <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-700 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                          Unverified
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">{rb.title}</h4>
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 line-clamp-2">{rb.description}</p>

                    {rb.commands && rb.commands.length > 0 && (
                      <div className="p-2.5 bg-slate-950 text-slate-200 rounded-lg font-mono text-[11px] flex items-center justify-between">
                        <span className="truncate pr-2">$ {rb.commands[0]}</span>
                        <button
                          onClick={() => {
                            navigator.clipboard.writeText(rb.commands[0]);
                            setCopiedCmd(rb.commands[0]);
                            setTimeout(() => setCopiedCmd(null), 2000);
                          }}
                          className="p-1 hover:bg-slate-800 rounded text-slate-400"
                        >
                          {copiedCmd === rb.commands[0] ? <Check className="w-3 h-3 text-emerald-400" /> : <Copy className="w-3 h-3" />}
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB 7: Acknowledgement & Quiz Receipt */}
          {activeTab === "receipt" && (
            <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-6 shadow-xs max-w-2xl mx-auto space-y-5">
              <div className="border-b border-slate-100 pb-3">
                <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">Successor Comprehension Check & Cryptographic Receipt</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Formal sign-off acknowledging understanding of project architecture and continuation plan.</p>
              </div>

              {!ackSubmitted ? (
                <div className="space-y-4">
                  <div className="p-3.5 bg-slate-50 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 space-y-2 text-xs">
                    <span className="font-bold text-slate-900 block">Comprehension Verification Check:</span>
                    <div className="space-y-1.5 text-slate-700">
                      <label className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <input type="checkbox" defaultChecked className="rounded text-blue-600" />
                        <span>I understand that micro-ROS on STM32 bridges with the ROS2 DDS daemon over serial UART.</span>
                      </label>
                      <label className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <input type="checkbox" defaultChecked className="rounded text-blue-600" />
                        <span>I acknowledge that production HashiCorp Vault root tokens must be unsealed with Admin Sarah.</span>
                      </label>
                      <label className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <input type="checkbox" defaultChecked className="rounded text-blue-600" />
                        <span>I have reviewed the Day 1 / Week 1 / Day 30 Continuation Plan and the Living Setup Runbook.</span>
                      </label>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <button
                    onClick={handleSubmitAcknowledgement}
                    className="w-full py-2.5 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold shadow-xs"
                  >
                    Digitally Sign & Generate Cryptographic Receipt Hash
                  </button>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="p-5 bg-emerald-50/70 border border-emerald-200 rounded-xl space-y-3 text-xs">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-emerald-800 font-bold">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-600" />
                    <span>Handover Acknowledgement Cryptographically Recorded</span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-slate-700 leading-relaxed">
                    Successor Sam Taylor has acknowledged receipt of Project Aether Knowledge Handover v1.0. This tamper-proof confirmation has been written to the append-only audit trail.
                  </p>
                  <div className="p-2.5 bg-white rounded-lg border border-emerald-200 font-mono text-[11px] text-slate-800 break-all">
                    SHA-256 Receipt: {receiptHash}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
