####################################################################################################
FILE 88: frontend/src/app/dashboard/ledger/page.tsx
ABSOLUTE PATH: C:\Users\Omkar\Downloads\Baton-main\frontend\src\app\dashboard\ledger\page.tsx
####################################################################################################

"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { api } from "@/lib/api";
import { Claim, ClaimStatus, ClaimCategory } from "@/lib/types";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/StatusBadge";
import { SensitivityBadge } from "@/components/SensitivityBadge";
import { 
  Layers, 
  SearchCode, 
  AlertTriangle, 
  HelpCircle, 
  Compass, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  XCircle, 
  ExternalLink, 
  ShieldCheck, 
  FileText,
  Search,
  Filter,
  Check,
  RefreshCw,
  X
} from "lucide-react";

const TABS = [
  { id: "ledger", name: "Claims Ledger", icon: Layers },
  { id: "evidence", name: "Split Evidence Viewer", icon: SearchCode },
  { id: "conflicts", name: "Conflicts & Gaps", icon: AlertTriangle },
  { id: "decisions", name: "Decision Archaeology", icon: Compass },
];

export default function KnowledgeLedgerPage() {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState("ledger");
  const [claims, setClaims] = useState<Claim[]>([]);
  const [decisions, setDecisions] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [projectId, setProjectId] = useState<string>("");

  // Filters
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<string>("all");
  const [categoryFilter, setCategoryFilter] = useState<string>("all");

  // Split-screen evidence selection
  const [selectedClaim, setSelectedClaim] = useState<Claim | null>(null);

  // 6-Stage Verification Modal State
  const [verificationModalClaim, setVerificationModalClaim] = useState<Claim | null>(null);
  const [verificationReport, setVerificationReport] = useState<any | null>(null);
  const [verifying, setVerifying] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    api.getProjects().then((projs) => {
      const pid = projs[0]?.id;
      if (pid) {
        setProjectId(pid);
        Promise.all([
          api.getClaims(pid),
          api.getDecisions(pid).catch(() => [])
        ]).then(([cls, decs]) => {
          setClaims(cls);
          setDecisions(decs);
          if (cls.length > 0) setSelectedClaim(cls[0]);
          setLoading(false);
        });
      } else {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    });
  }, []);

  const handleOpenVerification = async (claim: Claim) => {
    setVerificationModalClaim(claim);
    setVerifying(true);
    setVerificationReport(null);
    try {
      const report = await api.getClaimVerificationDetail(claim.id);
      setVerificationReport(report);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Verification failed:", err);
    } finally {
      setVerifying(false);
    }
  };

  const handleReverify = async () => {
    if (!verificationModalClaim) return;
    setVerifying(true);
    try {
      const report = await api.reverifyClaim(verificationModalClaim.id);
      setVerificationReport(report);
      // Update claim in list
      setClaims((prev) => prev.map((c) => c.id === verificationModalClaim.id ? { ...c, status: report.final_recommended_status } : c));
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Re-verification failed:", err);
    } finally {
      setVerifying(false);
    }
  };

  const filteredClaims = claims.filter((c) => {
    const matchesSearch = c.description.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) || 
                          (c.exact_quote && c.exact_quote.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()));
    const matchesStatus = statusFilter === "all" || c.status === statusFilter;
    const matchesCategory = categoryFilter === "all" || c.category === categoryFilter;
    return matchesSearch && matchesStatus && matchesCategory;
  });

  const conflictsAndGaps = claims.filter((c) => c.status === "Conflict" || c.status === "Missing");

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 max-w-7xl mx-auto">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-200 pb-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">Central Knowledge Ledger</h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
            Verified knowledge claims grounded in exact verbatim citations, multi-stage pipelines & decision logs.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Tab Controls */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-slate-100 p-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200">
          {TABS.map((tab) => {
            const Icon = tab.icon;
            const isActive = activeTab === tab.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={tab.id}
                onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.id)}
                className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md text-xs font-semibold transition-colors ${
                  isActive
                    ? "bg-white text-slate-900 shadow-xs"
                    : "text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900"
                }`}
              >
                <Icon className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${isActive ? "text-blue-600" : "text-slate-400"}`} />
                <span>{tab.name}</span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {loading ? (
        <div className="text-center py-20 text-slate-400 text-xs">
          <div className="w-6 h-6 border-2 border-blue-600 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto mb-2" />
          Loading Knowledge Ledger...
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          {/* TAB 1: Claims Ledger */}
          {activeTab === "ledger" && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {/* Filter Strip */}
              <div className="bg-white p-3.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-3">
                <div className="relative flex-1 max-w-md">
                  <Search className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="Search claims or exact quotation text..."
                    value={search}
                    onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full pl-8 pr-3 py-1.5 text-xs bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 font-medium"
                  />
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <select
                    value={statusFilter}
                    onChange={(e) => setStatusFilter(e.target.value)}
                    className="px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg shadow-xs font-medium text-slate-700"
                  >
                    <option value="all">All Statuses ({claims.length})</option>
                    <option value="Verified">Verified</option>
                    <option value="Evidence-backed">Evidence-backed</option>
                    <option value="Inferred">Inferred</option>
                    <option value="Conflict">Conflict</option>
                    <option value="Missing">Missing</option>
                    <option value="Unverified">Unverified</option>
                  </select>

                  <select
                    value={categoryFilter}
                    onChange={(e) => setCategoryFilter(e.target.value)}
                    className="px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg shadow-xs font-medium text-slate-700"
                  >
                    <option value="all">All Categories</option>
                    <option value="Decision">Decisions</option>
                    <option value="Task Ownership">Task Ownership</option>
                    <option value="Pending Task">Pending Tasks</option>
                    <option value="Dependency">Dependencies</option>
                    <option value="Blocker">Blockers</option>
                    <option value="Risk">Risks</option>
                    <option value="Deployment & Access">Deployment & Access</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Claims Table */}
              <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs overflow-hidden">
                <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                  <table className="w-full text-left text-xs">
                    <thead className="bg-slate-50 border-b border-slate-200 text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider text-[10px] font-bold">
                      <tr>
                        <th className="p-3.5">Category & Claim</th>
                        <th className="p-3.5">Exact Source Quotation</th>
                        <th className="p-3.5">Grounding Location</th>
                        <th className="p-3.5">Status</th>
                        <th className="p-3.5">Sensitivity</th>
                        <th className="p-3.5 text-right">Verification</th>
                      </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                      {filteredClaims.map((c) => (
                        <tr key={c.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/70 transition-colors">
                          <td className="p-3.5 max-w-xs space-y-1">
                            <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-slate-400 bg-slate-100 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">
                              {c.category}
                            </span>
                            <p className="font-semibold text-slate-900 leading-snug">
                              {c.description}
                            </p>
                          </td>

                          <td className="p-3.5 max-w-sm">
                            {c.exact_quote ? (
                              <blockquote className="text-[11px] text-slate-700 italic border-l-2 border-blue-400 pl-2 line-clamp-2">
                                "{c.exact_quote}"
                              </blockquote>
                            ) : (
                              <span className="text-slate-400 italic text-[11px]">No exact quotation (Inferred/Missing)</span>
                            )}
                          </td>

                          <td className="p-3.5 text-slate-500 font-mono text-[11px]">
                            {c.evidence_location || "Ledger record"}
                          </td>

                          <td className="p-3.5">
                            <StatusBadge status={c.status} />
                          </td>

                          <td className="p-3.5">
                            <SensitivityBadge level={c.sensitivity} />
                          </td>

                          <td className="p-3.5 text-right">
                            <button
                              onClick={() => handleOpenVerification(c)}
                              className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-slate-100 hover:bg-blue-50 hover:text-blue-700 text-slate-700 font-semibold text-[11px] border border-slate-200 transition-colors"
                            >
                              Verify Pipeline
                            </button>
                          </td>
                        </tr>
                      ))}
                    </tbody>
                  </table>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB 2: Split Evidence Viewer */}
          {activeTab === "evidence" && (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-6 h-[720px]">
              {/* Left Column: Claim Selector */}
              <div className="lg:col-span-5 bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
                <div className="p-3.5 border-b border-slate-100">
                  <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800">Select Claim to Trace Evidence</h3>
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto divide-y divide-slate-100 p-2 space-y-1">
                  {claims.map((c) => (
                    <div
                      key={c.id}
                      onClick={() => setSelectedClaim(c)}
                      className={`p-3 rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-all ${
                        selectedClaim?.id === c.id
                          ? "bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 shadow-xs"
                          : "hover:bg-slate-50 border border-transparent"
                      }`}
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-1">
                        <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase">{c.category}</span>
                        <StatusBadge status={c.status} />
                      </div>
                      <p className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-800 line-clamp-2">{c.description}</p>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Right Column: Source Document Viewer & Exact Span Highlight */}
              <div className="lg:col-span-7 bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
                <div className="p-3.5 border-b border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <FileText className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-600" />
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900">
                      Grounded Evidence Inspector
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  {selectedClaim && (
                    <button
                      onClick={() => handleOpenVerification(selectedClaim)}
                      className="text-xs text-blue-600 hover:underline font-semibold"
                    >
                      View 6-Stage Proof →
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>

                {selectedClaim ? (
                  <div className="p-5 flex-1 overflow-y-auto space-y-4">
                    <div className="space-y-1">
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-slate-400">Selected Claim:</span>
                      <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">{selectedClaim.description}</h4>
                    </div>

                    <div className="p-3.5 bg-slate-50 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 space-y-2 text-xs">
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-500">
                        <span>Evidence Location:</span>
                        <span className="font-mono font-semibold text-slate-800">{selectedClaim.evidence_location || "N/A"}</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-500">
                        <span>Confidence Score:</span>
                        <span className="font-mono font-bold text-emerald-600">{Math.round(selectedClaim.confidence * 100)}% Grounded</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 block mb-1.5">
                        Verbatim Source Span (Exact Match):
                      </span>
                      {selectedClaim.exact_quote ? (
                        <div className="p-4 bg-amber-50/70 border border-amber-200 rounded-xl text-xs text-slate-900 leading-relaxed font-mono">
                          <span className="bg-amber-200 font-semibold px-1 rounded">
                            "{selectedClaim.exact_quote}"
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      ) : (
                        <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 italic">No exact source quote available for this claim.</p>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <div className="p-12 text-center text-xs text-slate-400">Select a claim from the left panel.</div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB 3: Conflicts & Knowledge Gaps */}
          {activeTab === "conflicts" && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div className="p-4 bg-amber-50/80 border border-amber-200 rounded-xl text-xs text-amber-900 leading-relaxed">
                <span className="font-bold">Principle of Preserved Uncertainty: </span>
                BATON never silently averages contradictory claims. Opposing evidence and missing information remain visibly flagged until explicitly reconciled.
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                {conflictsAndGaps.map((item) => (
                  <div key={item.id} className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 shadow-xs space-y-3">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                        {item.category}
                      </span>
                      <StatusBadge status={item.status} />
                    </div>

                    <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 leading-snug">{item.description}</h4>

                    {item.exact_quote && (
                      <blockquote className="text-xs text-slate-700 italic border-l-2 border-rose-400 pl-2">
                        "{item.exact_quote}"
                      </blockquote>
                    )}

                    <div className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                      <span className="text-slate-400 font-mono text-[11px]">{item.evidence_location || "Ledger record"}</span>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => handleOpenVerification(item)}
                        className="text-blue-600 hover:underline font-semibold"
                      >
                        Run Verification →
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB 4: Decision Archaeology */}
          {activeTab === "decisions" && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {decisions.map((dec) => (
                <div key={dec.id} className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-5 shadow-xs space-y-3">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900">{dec.title}</span>
                      <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold px-2 py-0.2 rounded border bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200">
                        {dec.reversal_status}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    {dec.decided_date && <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono">{dec.decided_date}</span>}
                  </div>

                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-700 leading-relaxed font-medium">
                    <span className="text-slate-500 font-normal">What was decided: </span>{dec.what_decided}
                  </p>

                  <div className="p-3 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg border border-slate-100 text-xs text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">
                    <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">Rationale: </span>{dec.why_selected}
                  </div>

                  {dec.alternatives_considered && dec.alternatives_considered.length > 0 && (
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
                      <span className="text-slate-500 font-medium">Alternatives Considered:</span>
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1">
                        {dec.alternatives_considered.map((alt: string, i: number) => (
                          <span key={i} className="bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px]">
                            {alt}
                          </span>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}

      {/* 6-STAGE CLAIM VERIFICATION MODAL */}
      {verificationModalClaim && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-950/60 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-2xl max-w-2xl w-full p-6 space-y-5 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
            {/* Modal Header */}
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between border-b border-slate-100 pb-3">
              <div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                    Claim ID: {verificationModalClaim.id.substring(0, 8)}
                  </span>
                  <StatusBadge status={verificationModalClaim.status} />
                </div>
                <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 mt-1">
                  Multi-Stage Verification Diagnostic
                </h3>
              </div>

              <button
                onClick={() => setVerificationModalClaim(null)}
                className="p-1 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600"
              >
                <X className="w-5 h-5" />
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Claim Summary */}
            <div className="p-3.5 bg-slate-50 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 space-y-1 text-xs">
              <span className="font-semibold text-slate-500">Claim Description:</span>
              <p className="font-bold text-slate-900">{verificationModalClaim.description}</p>
            </div>

            {/* 6 Diagnostic Stages */}
            {verifying ? (
              <div className="text-center py-10 text-xs text-slate-400">
                <div className="w-5 h-5 border-2 border-blue-600 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto mb-2" />
                Executing 6-stage deterministic verification pipeline...
              </div>
            ) : verificationReport?.stages ? (
              <div className="space-y-3">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500 block">
                  Pipeline Diagnostic Results
                </span>

                {Object.entries(verificationReport.stages).map(([stageKey, stage]: [string, any], idx) => (
                  <div key={stageKey} className="p-3 rounded-xl border bg-slate-50/50 flex items-start gap-3 text-xs">
                    <div className="mt-0.5">
                      {stage.passed ? (
                        <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600" />
                      ) : (
                        <XCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-600" />
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <div className="space-y-0.5 flex-1">
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                        <span className="font-bold text-slate-900">
                          Stage {idx + 1}: {stage.name}
                        </span>
                        <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold uppercase px-2 py-0.2 rounded ${
                          stage.passed ? "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700" : "bg-rose-50 text-rose-700"
                        }`}>
                          {stage.passed ? "PASSED" : "FAILED"}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <p className="text-slate-600 leading-snug">{stage.details}</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : null}

            {/* Modal Footer Controls */}
            <div className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                Recommended Status: <strong className="text-slate-900">{verificationReport?.final_recommended_status || "Evaluating..."}</strong>
              </span>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <button
                  onClick={handleReverify}
                  disabled={verifying}
                  className="px-3.5 py-1.5 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold shadow-xs disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {verifying ? "Verifying..." : "Re-Execute Pipeline"}
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => setVerificationModalClaim(null)}
                  className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold"
                >
                  Close
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

####################################################################################################

"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { api } from "@/lib/api";
import { 
  Target, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  Clock, 
  Circle, 
  FileEdit, 
  Save, 
  ArrowRight, 
  Compass, 
  Terminal, 
  ShieldCheck,
  Sparkles
} from "lucide-react";

interface Mission {
  id: string;
  project_id: string;
  mission_number: number;
  title: string;
  objective: string;
  estimated_minutes: number;
  instructions_markdown: string;
  completion_criteria: string[];
  status: string; // pending, in_progress, completed
  successor_notes?: string;
  completed_at?: string;
}

export default function MissionsPage() {
  const [missions, setMissions] = useState<Mission[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [projectId, setProjectId] = useState<string>("");
  const [notesState, setNotesState] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [savingId, setSavingId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    api.getProjects().then((projs) => {
      const pid = projs[0]?.id;
      if (pid) {
        setProjectId(pid);
        api.getMissions(pid).then((data) => {
          setMissions(data);
          const initialNotes: Record<string, string> = {};
          data.forEach((m: Mission) => {
            initialNotes[m.id] = m.successor_notes || "";
          });
          setNotesState(initialNotes);
          setLoading(false);
        });
      } else {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    });
  }, []);

  const handleStatusToggle = async (mission: Mission) => {
    if (!projectId) return;
    const nextStatus = mission.status === "completed" 
      ? "pending" 
      : mission.status === "pending" 
      ? "in_progress" 
      : "completed";

    try {
      const updated = await api.updateMission(projectId, mission.id, {
        status: nextStatus,
        successor_notes: notesState[mission.id]
      });
      setMissions((prev) => prev.map((m) => (m.id === mission.id ? updated : m)));
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Failed to update mission status:", err);
    }
  };

  const handleSaveNotes = async (missionId: string) => {
    if (!projectId) return;
    setSavingId(missionId);
    try {
      const updated = await api.updateMission(projectId, missionId, {
        successor_notes: notesState[missionId]
      });
      setMissions((prev) => prev.map((m) => (m.id === missionId ? updated : m)));
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Failed to save notes:", err);
    } finally {
      setSavingId(null);
    }
  };

  const completedCount = missions.filter((m) => m.status === "completed").length;
  const progressPercent = missions.length > 0 ? Math.round((completedCount / missions.length) * 100) : 0;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">Successor Mission Mode</h1>
            <span className="text-xs bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 font-semibold px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-amber-200">
              Interactive Onboarding
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
            Turn passive handover reading into 6 guided operational missions to assume command of Project Aether safely.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Progress Metric Banner */}
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl shadow-sm flex items-center gap-4">
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 text-xs">
              <span className="text-slate-500 font-medium">Onboarding Velocity</span>
              <span className="font-bold text-slate-900">{completedCount} of {missions.length} Missions</span>
            </div>
            <div className="w-40 bg-slate-100 rounded-full h-2 overflow-hidden">
              <div 
                className="bg-emerald-500 h-2 rounded-full transition-all duration-500" 
                style={{ width: `${progressPercent}%` }}
              />
            </div>
          </div>
          <span className="text-lg font-extrabold text-emerald-600">{progressPercent}%</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Missions Accordion / List */}
      {loading ? (
        <div className="text-center py-20 text-slate-400 text-sm">
          <div className="w-6 h-6 border-2 border-amber-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto mb-2" />
          Loading guided successor missions...
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {missions.map((m) => {
            const isCompleted = m.status === "completed";
            const isInProgress = m.status === "in_progress";

            return (
              <div
                key={m.id}
                className={`bg-white rounded-2xl border transition-all shadow-sm overflow-hidden ${
                  isCompleted 
                    ? "border-emerald-300 ring-1 ring-emerald-200" 
                    : isInProgress
                    ? "border-amber-300 ring-1 ring-amber-200"
                    : "border-slate-200"
                }`}
              >
                {/* Top Mission Strip */}
                <div className="p-5 flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4 bg-slate-50/60 border-b border-slate-100">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5">
                    <button
                      onClick={() => handleStatusToggle(m)}
                      title="Click to cycle status"
                      className="transition-transform hover:scale-110 flex-shrink-0"
                    >
                      {isCompleted ? (
                        <CheckCircle2 className="w-7 h-7 text-emerald-500 fill-emerald-100" />
                      ) : isInProgress ? (
                        <Clock className="w-7 h-7 text-amber-500 animate-spin" style={{ animationDuration: "10s" }} />
                      ) : (
                        <Circle className="w-7 h-7 text-slate-300" />
                      )}
                    </button>

                    <div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">
                          Mission 0{m.mission_number}
                        </span>
                        <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold uppercase px-2 py-0.2 rounded border ${
                          isCompleted
                            ? "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200"
                            : isInProgress
                            ? "bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200"
                            : "bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 border-slate-200"
                        }`}>
                          {m.status.replace("_", " ")}
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono">
                          ~{m.estimated_minutes} min
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 mt-0.5">
                        {m.title}
                      </h3>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <button
                    onClick={() => handleStatusToggle(m)}
                    className={`text-xs font-semibold px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border shadow-sm transition-colors ${
                      isCompleted
                        ? "bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-200"
                        : isInProgress
                        ? "bg-emerald-600 text-white border-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700"
                        : "bg-amber-500 text-white border-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {isCompleted ? "Mark Incomplete" : isInProgress ? "Mark Completed ✓" : "Start Mission →"}
                  </button>
                </div>

                {/* Mission Content */}
                <div className="p-5 space-y-4">
                  <div>
                    <h4 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 mb-1">
                      Mission Objective
                    </h4>
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-700 leading-relaxed font-medium">
                      {m.objective}
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  {/* Instructions */}
                  <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100 space-y-2">
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500 block">
                      Instructions & Walkthrough
                    </span>
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line">
                      {m.instructions_markdown}
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  {/* Completion Criteria Checklist */}
                  <div className="space-y-1.5">
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 block">
                      Verification Criteria
                    </span>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                      {m.completion_criteria.map((crit, idx) => (
                        <div key={idx} className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100 text-xs text-slate-700 flex items-start gap-2">
                          <CheckCircle2 className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5 ${
                            isCompleted ? "text-emerald-500" : "text-slate-400"
                          }`} />
                          <span>{crit}</span>
                        </div>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Successor Notes & Findings Field */}
                  <div className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-100 space-y-2">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                      <span className="font-bold text-slate-700 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                        <FileEdit className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-blue-600" />
                        Successor Log & Observations
                      </span>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => handleSaveNotes(m.id)}
                        disabled={savingId === m.id}
                        className="text-xs text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 font-semibold flex items-center gap-1"
                      >
                        <Save className="w-3 h-3" />
                        <span>{savingId === m.id ? "Saving..." : "Save Notes"}</span>
                      </button>
                    </div>

                    <textarea
                      rows={2}
                      placeholder="Record your test results, branch names verified, or questions for Alex / Elena..."
                      value={notesState[m.id] || ""}
                      onChange={(e) => setNotesState({ ...notesState, [m.id]: e.target.value })}
                      className="w-full p-2.5 text-xs bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 focus:border-blue-500"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { api } from "@/lib/api";
import { AnalyticsData } from "@/lib/types";
import { 
  FolderGit2, 
  FileSpreadsheet, 
  CheckSquare, 
  AlertTriangle, 
  ShieldCheck, 
  Layers, 
  ArrowRight,
  Activity,
  CheckCircle2,
  Clock,
  Compass,
  FileText
} from "lucide-react";

export default function OverviewPage() {
  const [data, setData] = useState<AnalyticsData | null>(null);
  const [readiness, setReadiness] = useState<any>(null);
  const [risks, setRisks] = useState<any>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    Promise.all([
      api.getAnalytics(),
      api.getProjects()
    ])
      .then(([analyticsRes, projs]) => {
        setData(analyticsRes);
        const pid = projs[0]?.id;
        if (pid) {
          Promise.all([
            api.getReadiness(pid).catch(() => null),
            api.getRiskRadar(pid).catch(() => null)
          ]).then(([rData, riskData]) => {
            setReadiness(rData);
            setRisks(riskData);
            setLoading(false);
          });
        } else {
          setLoading(false);
        }
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        console.error(err);
        setLoading(false);
      });
  }, []);

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center min-h-[400px]">
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3">
          <div className="w-6 h-6 border-2 border-blue-600 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
          <p className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">Loading platform overview...</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const totalClaims = (data?.verified_claims || 0) + (data?.evidence_backed_claims || 0) + (data?.conflicting_claims || 0) + (data?.missing_information || 0) + (data?.inferred_claims || 0);
  const coveragePercent = totalClaims > 0 ? Math.round(((data?.verified_claims || 0) / totalClaims) * 100) : 0;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 max-w-7xl mx-auto">
      {/* Page Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-200 pb-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">Executive Handover Overview</h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
            Real-time status of project knowledge continuity, risk mitigation, and verification readiness.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Link
            href="/dashboard/handovers"
            className="px-3.5 py-1.5 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white text-xs font-semibold rounded-lg shadow-xs transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5"
          >
            <span>Handover Center</span>
            <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Active Project Banner */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-5 shadow-xs flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div className="space-y-1">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
              Active Handover
            </span>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">•</span>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium">Outgoing: Alex Mercer → Incoming: Sam Taylor</span>
          </div>
          <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
            Project Aether – Autonomous Robotics Platform
          </h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 max-w-2xl leading-relaxed">
            Autonomous ground vehicle initiative migrating from ROS1 Noetic to ROS2 Humble with Cartographer 3D SLAM.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Readiness Score Pill */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4 bg-slate-50 p-3.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 flex-shrink-0">
          <div className="text-right">
            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 uppercase font-bold block">Readiness Score</span>
            <span className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">
              {readiness?.overall_score || 53.6}<span className="text-xs font-normal text-slate-400">/100</span>
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold border ${
            readiness?.readiness_grade === "Ready"
              ? "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-300"
              : readiness?.readiness_grade === "Blocked"
              ? "bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border-rose-300"
              : "bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-300"
          }`}>
            {readiness?.readiness_grade || "Blocked"}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 4 Primary Summary Cards */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        {/* 1. Knowledge Coverage */}
        <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs space-y-2">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-400">
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider">Knowledge Coverage</span>
            <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600" />
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between">
            <span className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">{coveragePercent}%</span>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium">{data?.verified_claims} / {totalClaims} claims</span>
          </div>
          <div className="w-full bg-slate-100 rounded-full h-1.5 overflow-hidden">
            <div className="bg-emerald-500 h-1.5 rounded-full" style={{ width: `${coveragePercent}%` }} />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 2. Critical Risks */}
        <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs space-y-2">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-400">
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider">Critical Risks</span>
            <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-500" />
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between">
            <span className="text-2xl font-bold text-rose-600">{risks?.critical_count || 2}</span>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium">{risks?.total_risks || 8} total tracked</span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 truncate">Kafka broker & Vault root token silos</p>
        </div>

        {/* 3. Pending Reviews */}
        <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs space-y-2">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-400">
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider">Pending Reviews</span>
            <CheckSquare className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500" />
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between">
            <span className="text-2xl font-bold text-amber-600">{data?.pending_reviews || 0}</span>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium">Awaiting reviewer</span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 truncate">Requires Elena Rostova sign-off</p>
        </div>

        {/* 4. Active Handovers */}
        <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs space-y-2">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-400">
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider">Active Handovers</span>
            <FileSpreadsheet className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-600" />
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between">
            <span className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">{data?.active_handovers || 1}</span>
            <span className="text-xs text-emerald-600 font-medium">Version 1.0</span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 truncate">Role views generated & secured</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Two Column Layout: Readiness Breakdown & Critical Blockers */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
        {/* Left: 6-Pillar Readiness Score Breakdown */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-5 shadow-xs space-y-4">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 pb-3">
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">Handover Readiness Pillars</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Deterministic 6-factor continuity evaluation</p>
            </div>
            <Link href="/dashboard/handovers" className="text-xs text-blue-600 hover:underline font-medium">
              View Handover →
            </Link>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-3">
            {readiness?.categories?.map((cat: any, idx: number) => {
              const pct = Math.round((cat.score / cat.max_score) * 100);
              return (
                <div key={idx} className="space-y-1">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                    <span className="font-medium text-slate-700">{cat.category}</span>
                    <span className="font-semibold text-slate-900">
                      {cat.score} / {cat.max_score} <span className="text-slate-400 font-normal">({pct}%)</span>
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="w-full bg-slate-100 rounded-full h-1.5 overflow-hidden">
                    <div
                      className={`h-1.5 rounded-full ${pct >= 80 ? "bg-emerald-500" : pct >= 60 ? "bg-blue-500" : "bg-amber-500"}`}
                      style={{ width: `${pct}%` }}
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Right: Critical Continuity Risks & Priority Actions */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-5 shadow-xs space-y-4">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 pb-3">
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">Critical Continuity Risks</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Must be addressed prior to leaver departure</p>
            </div>
            <Link href="/dashboard/ledger" className="text-xs text-blue-600 hover:underline font-medium">
              View Ledger →
            </Link>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-3">
            {risks?.findings?.slice(0, 3).map((r: any) => (
              <div key={r.id} className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-1.5">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 leading-snug">{r.title}</span>
                  <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold uppercase px-2 py-0.2 rounded border ${
                    r.severity === "critical"
                      ? "bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border-rose-200"
                      : "bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200"
                  }`}>
                    {r.severity}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-600 leading-relaxed line-clamp-2">
                  {r.description}
                </p>
                <div className="text-[11px] text-blue-700 font-medium">
                  <span className="font-semibold">Mitigation: </span>{r.mitigation}
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Recent Activity / Audit Trail Feed */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-5 shadow-xs space-y-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 pb-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Activity className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-600" />
            <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">Recent Audit Trail</h3>
          </div>
          <Link href="/dashboard/security" className="text-xs font-semibold text-blue-600 hover:underline">
            Security & Audit Center →
          </Link>
        </div>

        <div className="divide-y divide-slate-100 text-xs">
          {(data?.recent_activity || []).slice(0, 5).map((act) => (
            <div key={act.id} className="py-2.5 flex items-center justify-between">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-blue-600" />
                <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800 capitalize">
                  {act.action.replace(/_/g, " ")}
                </span>
                <span className="text-slate-400">•</span>
                <span className="text-slate-600">{act.user}</span>
              </div>
              <span className="text-slate-400 font-mono text-[11px]">{act.time}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState, useMemo, useRef } from "react";
import { api } from "@/lib/api";
import { 
  FolderGit2, 
  FileStack, 
  Network, 
  Clock, 
  Users, 
  Upload, 
  ShieldCheck, 
  Search, 
  ExternalLink,
  ZoomIn,
  ZoomOut,
  RotateCcw,
  CheckCircle2,
  AlertTriangle,
  FileText,
  Info
} from "lucide-react";
import { SensitivityBadge } from "@/components/SensitivityBadge";

interface Project {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  slug: string;
  description?: string;
  repository_url?: string;
  total_sources: number;
  total_claims: number;
  total_handovers: number;
}

interface Source {
  id: string;
  filename: string;
  file_type: string;
  sensitivity: string;
  consent_confirmed: boolean;
  created_at: string;
}

const TABS = [
  { id: "overview", name: "Project & Team", icon: FolderGit2 },
  { id: "sources", name: "Sources & Ingestion", icon: FileStack },
  { id: "graph", name: "Contextual Knowledge Graph", icon: Network },
  { id: "timeline", name: "Timeline & Milestones", icon: Clock },
];

export default function ProjectsModulePage() {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState("overview");
  const [project, setProject] = useState<Project | null>(null);
  const [sources, setSources] = useState<Source[]>([]);
  const [timeline, setTimeline] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  // Graph state
  const [graphNodes, setGraphNodes] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [graphEdges, setGraphEdges] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [graphStats, setGraphStats] = useState<Record<string, number>>({});
  const [graphSearch, setGraphSearch] = useState("");
  const [graphFilter, setGraphFilter] = useState("all");
  const [selectedNode, setSelectedNode] = useState<any | null>(null);
  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1);
  const [pan, setPan] = useState({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false);
  const dragStart = useRef({ x: 0, y: 0 });

  useEffect(() => {
    api.getProjects().then((projs) => {
      const p = projs[0];
      if (p) {
        setProject(p);
        Promise.all([
          api.getSources(p.id).catch(() => []),
          api.getTimeline(p.id).catch(() => []),
          api.getKnowledgeGraph(p.id).catch(() => ({ nodes: [], edges: [], stats: {} }))
        ]).then(([srcs, tl, gData]) => {
          setSources(srcs);
          setTimeline(tl);
          
          // Layout graph nodes
          const total = gData.nodes.length;
          const positioned = gData.nodes.map((node: any, i: number) => {
            let radius = 240;
            if (node.type === "system") radius = 100;
            else if (node.type === "person") radius = 170;
            else if (node.type === "decision") radius = 260;
            else if (node.type === "risk") radius = 340;
            else if (node.type === "task") radius = 420;
            else radius = 490;

            const angle = (i / total) * 2 * Math.PI;
            const x = 550 + Math.cos(angle) * (radius + (i % 3) * 30);
            const y = 400 + Math.sin(angle) * (radius + (i % 2) * 25);
            return { ...node, x, y };
          });
          setGraphNodes(positioned);
          setGraphEdges(gData.edges);
          setGraphStats(gData.stats || {});
          if (positioned.length > 0) setSelectedNode(positioned[0]);

          setLoading(false);
        });
      } else {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    });
  }, []);

  const filteredNodes = useMemo(() => {
    return graphNodes.filter((n) => {
      const matchSearch = n.label.toLowerCase().includes(graphSearch.toLowerCase()) ||
                          (n.details && n.details.toLowerCase().includes(graphSearch.toLowerCase()));
      const matchFilter = graphFilter === "all" || n.type === graphFilter;
      return matchSearch && matchFilter;
    });
  }, [graphNodes, graphSearch, graphFilter]);

  const filteredNodeIds = useMemo(() => new Set(filteredNodes.map((n) => n.id)), [filteredNodes]);
  const visibleEdges = useMemo(() => {
    return graphEdges.filter((e) => filteredNodeIds.has(e.source) && filteredNodeIds.has(e.target));
  }, [graphEdges, filteredNodeIds]);

  const nodeMap = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, any>();
    graphNodes.forEach((n) => map.set(n.id, n));
    return map;
  }, [graphNodes]);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 max-w-7xl mx-auto">
      {/* Module Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-200 pb-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">Project Management & Architecture</h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
            Manage repository exports, multi-format sources, members, milestones, and architectural topology.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Tab Controls */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-slate-100 p-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200">
          {TABS.map((tab) => {
            const Icon = tab.icon;
            const isActive = activeTab === tab.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={tab.id}
                onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.id)}
                className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md text-xs font-semibold transition-colors ${
                  isActive
                    ? "bg-white text-slate-900 shadow-xs"
                    : "text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900"
                }`}
              >
                <Icon className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${isActive ? "text-blue-600" : "text-slate-400"}`} />
                <span>{tab.name}</span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {loading ? (
        <div className="text-center py-20 text-slate-400 text-xs">
          <div className="w-6 h-6 border-2 border-blue-600 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto mb-2" />
          Loading project data...
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          {/* TAB 1: Overview & Team Members */}
          {activeTab === "overview" && (
            <div className="space-y-6">
              <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-6 shadow-xs space-y-4">
                <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-100 pb-4">
                  <div>
                    <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">{project?.name}</h2>
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">{project?.description}</p>
                  </div>
                  {project?.repository_url && (
                    <a
                      href={project.repository_url}
                      target="_blank"
                      rel="noopener noreferrer"
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-blue-600 hover:underline font-medium"
                    >
                      <FolderGit2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      <span>{project.repository_url}</span>
                    </a>
                  )}
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4 text-xs pt-1">
                  <div className="p-3 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg border border-slate-100">
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase block">Sources Ingested</span>
                    <span className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">{sources.length}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="p-3 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg border border-slate-100">
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase block">Reconstructed Claims</span>
                    <span className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">{project?.total_claims || 38}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="p-3 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg border border-slate-100">
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase block">Active Handovers</span>
                    <span className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">{project?.total_handovers || 1}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="p-3 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg border border-slate-100">
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase block">Target Architecture</span>
                    <span className="text-base font-bold text-blue-700">ROS2 Humble</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Team Members List */}
              <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs overflow-hidden">
                <div className="p-4 border-b border-slate-100">
                  <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">Project Team & Role-Based Clearance</h3>
                </div>
                <div className="divide-y divide-slate-100 text-xs">
                  {[
                    { name: "Sarah Chen", email: "admin@baton.local", role: "Admin", clearance: "Restricted (All Levels)", status: "Active" },
                    { name: "Alex Mercer", email: "alex.leaver@baton.local", role: "Outgoing Leaver", clearance: "Restricted", status: "Transitioning" },
                    { name: "Elena Rostova", email: "elena.reviewer@baton.local", role: "Tech Reviewer", clearance: "Restricted", status: "Active" },
                    { name: "Sam Taylor", email: "sam.successor@baton.local", role: "Successor", clearance: "Confidential (Pre-Auth)", status: "Onboarding" },
                    { name: "Jordan Lee", email: "guest.viewer@baton.local", role: "Restricted Viewer", clearance: "Internal/Public Only", status: "Auditing" },
                  ].map((m, idx) => (
                    <div key={idx} className="p-4 flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-3">
                      <div>
                        <div className="font-bold text-slate-900">{m.name}</div>
                        <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 font-mono">{m.email}</div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                        <span className="text-xs bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 font-semibold px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded">
                          {m.role}
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-600 font-medium">Clearance: {m.clearance}</span>
                        <span className="text-[11px] text-emerald-600 font-semibold">{m.status}</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB 2: Sources & Ingestion */}
          {activeTab === "sources" && (
            <div className="space-y-6">
              <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-5 shadow-xs space-y-4">
                <div className="border-2 border-dashed border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-6 text-center space-y-2 bg-slate-50/50">
                  <Upload className="w-8 h-8 text-slate-400 mx-auto" />
                  <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800">Upload Authoritative Project Sources</h4>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 max-w-md mx-auto leading-relaxed">
                    Supported: GitHub exports (.json, .zip), Markdown (.md), Meeting Notes (.pdf), Task Boards (.csv, .json), Chat exports (.json).
                  </p>
                  <div className="pt-2 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-600">
                    <input type="checkbox" defaultChecked disabled className="rounded text-blue-600" />
                    <span>Consent confirmed: Source data authorized for project continuity extraction.</span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Sources List */}
                <div className="divide-y divide-slate-100 text-xs">
                  {sources.map((s) => (
                    <div key={s.id} className="py-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <FileText className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-600" />
                        <div>
                          <span className="font-semibold text-slate-900 block">{s.filename}</span>
                          <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-mono">Type: {s.file_type.toUpperCase()} • ID: {s.id.substring(0, 8)}</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <SensitivityBadge level={s.sensitivity as any} />
                        <span className="text-[11px] text-emerald-600 font-medium flex items-center gap-1">
                          <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Scanned & Redacted
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB 3: Contextual Knowledge Graph */}
          {activeTab === "graph" && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {/* Graph Toolbar */}
              <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-3">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 flex-1">
                  <div className="relative flex-1 max-w-xs">
                    <Search className="absolute left-2.5 top-2 w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      placeholder="Search nodes..."
                      value={graphSearch}
                      onChange={(e) => setGraphSearch(e.target.value)}
                      className="w-full pl-8 pr-3 py-1 text-xs bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                    {["all", "person", "decision", "task", "system", "risk"].map((t) => (
                      <button
                        key={t}
                        onClick={() => setGraphFilter(t)}
                        className={`px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold rounded capitalize ${
                          graphFilter === t ? "bg-slate-900 text-white" : "bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200"
                        }`}
                      >
                        {t}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-1 border-l border-slate-200 pl-2">
                  <button onClick={() => setZoom((z) => Math.min(2.0, z + 0.2))} className="p-1 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded text-slate-600">
                    <ZoomIn className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </button>
                  <button onClick={() => setZoom((z) => Math.max(0.5, z - 0.2))} className="p-1 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded text-slate-600">
                    <ZoomOut className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </button>
                  <button onClick={() => { setZoom(1); setPan({ x: 0, y: 0 }); }} className="p-1 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded text-slate-600">
                    <RotateCcw className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Graph Visual Area */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
                <div
                  className="lg:col-span-3 h-[580px] bg-slate-950 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 shadow-xs relative overflow-hidden select-none cursor-grab active:cursor-grabbing"
                  onMouseDown={(e) => {
                    setIsDragging(true);
                    dragStart.current = { x: e.clientX - pan.x, y: e.clientY - pan.y };
                  }}
                  onMouseMove={(e) => {
                    if (!isDragging) return;
                    setPan({ x: e.clientX - dragStart.current.x, y: e.clientY - dragStart.current.y });
                  }}
                  onMouseUp={() => setIsDragging(false)}
                  onMouseLeave={() => setIsDragging(false)}
                >
                  <svg
                    className="w-full h-full"
                    viewBox="0 0 1100 800"
                    style={{
                      transform: `translate(${pan.x}px, ${pan.y}px) scale(${zoom})`,
                      transformOrigin: "center center",
                      transition: isDragging ? "none" : "transform 0.1s ease-out"
                    }}
                  >
                    {/* Edges */}
                    {visibleEdges.map((e, idx) => {
                      const src = nodeMap.get(e.source);
                      const tgt = nodeMap.get(e.target);
                      if (!src || !tgt) return null;
                      const isSel = selectedNode && (e.source === selectedNode.id || e.target === selectedNode.id);
                      return (
                        <line
                          key={idx}
                          x1={src.x}
                          y1={src.y}
                          x2={tgt.x}
                          y2={tgt.y}
                          stroke={isSel ? "#3b82f6" : "#334155"}
                          strokeWidth={isSel ? 2 : 1}
                          opacity={isSel ? 1 : 0.4}
                        />
                      );
                    })}

                    {/* Nodes */}
                    {filteredNodes.map((n) => {
                      const isSel = selectedNode?.id === n.id;
                      return (
                        <g
                          key={n.id}
                          transform={`translate(${n.x}, ${n.y})`}
                          onClick={() => setSelectedNode(n)}
                          className="cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          <circle
                            r={n.type === "system" ? 18 : 14}
                            fill={isSel ? "#1e293b" : "#0f172a"}
                            stroke={isSel ? "#60a5fa" : n.type === "risk" ? "#f43f5e" : "#3b82f6"}
                            strokeWidth={isSel ? 3 : 1.5}
                          />
                          <text
                            y="24"
                            fill={isSel ? "#ffffff" : "#cbd5e1"}
                            fontSize="9"
                            fontWeight={isSel ? "700" : "500"}
                            textAnchor="middle"
                            className="pointer-events-none select-none"
                          >
                            {n.label}
                          </text>
                        </g>
                      );
                    })}
                  </svg>

                  <div className="absolute bottom-3 left-3 bg-slate-900/90 text-[10px] text-slate-300 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-800">
                    Click node to view evidence • Pan & Zoom active
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Node Inspector Drawer */}
                <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 shadow-xs space-y-3">
                  <div className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 border-b border-slate-100 pb-2">
                    Node Evidence Inspector
                  </div>
                  {selectedNode ? (
                    <div className="space-y-3">
                      <div>
                        <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                          {selectedNode.type}
                        </span>
                        <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 mt-1.5">{selectedNode.label}</h4>
                        {selectedNode.details && (
                          <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 mt-1 leading-relaxed">{selectedNode.details}</p>
                        )}
                      </div>

                      {selectedNode.evidence_quote && (
                        <div className="p-3 bg-blue-50/60 rounded-lg border border-blue-200 space-y-1">
                          <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-blue-700 block">
                            Grounded Quote:
                          </span>
                          <blockquote className="text-xs text-slate-800 italic">
                            "{selectedNode.evidence_quote}"
                          </blockquote>
                          {selectedNode.evidence_location && (
                            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-mono block pt-1">
                              Location: {selectedNode.evidence_location}
                            </span>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 text-center py-10">Select a node to inspect evidence.</p>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB 4: Timeline */}
          {activeTab === "timeline" && (
            <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-5 shadow-xs space-y-4">
              <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 border-b border-slate-100 pb-3">
                Chronological Milestone Timeline
              </h3>
              <div className="space-y-4">
                {(timeline.length > 0 ? timeline : [
                  { event: "Adopt micro-ROS on STM32", date: "2026-09-12", role: "Architecture" },
                  { event: "Select Livox Mid-360 LiDAR", date: "2026-09-18", role: "Sensors" },
                  { event: "Cartographer 3D SLAM Migration", date: "2026-10-02", role: "Navigation" },
                  { event: "Kafka Ingestion Discovered", date: "2026-10-15", role: "Telemetry" },
                  { event: "ROS1 Noetic Fallback Frozen", date: "2026-10-20", role: "Release" },
                ]).map((t, idx) => (
                  <div key={idx} className="flex items-start gap-3 text-xs">
                    <div className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-blue-600 mt-1.5 flex-shrink-0" />
                    <div className="flex-1">
                      <span className="font-bold text-slate-900">{t.event}</span>
                      <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 font-mono mt-0.5">
                        {t.date} • {t.role}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";

  Gauge,

} from "lucide-react";

interface CategoryScore {
  category: string;
  weight: number;
  score: number;
  max_score: number;
  status: string;
  rationale: string;
}

interface DeductionDetail {
  category: string;
  points_deducted: number;
  reason: string;
  remediation_action: string;
}

interface ReadinessData {
  project_id: string;
  overall_score: number;
  readiness_grade: string;
  is_deployable: boolean;
  critical_blockers_count: number;
  unresolved_conflicts_count: number;
  missing_gaps_count: number;
  categories: CategoryScore[];
  deductions: DeductionDetail[];
  recommended_actions: string[];
}

export default function ReadinessScorePage() {
  const [data, setData] = useState<ReadinessData | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    api.getProjects().then((projs) => {

      if (pid) {
        api.getReadiness(pid).then((res) => {
          setData(res);
          setLoading(false);
        });
      } else {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    });
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Header */}

              Deterministic 0–100 Formula
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
            Auditable, evidence-backed evaluation computing whether incoming successors can assume operational control safely.
          </p>
        </div>

        <Link
          href="/dashboard/missions"
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 text-white text-xs font-semibold rounded-xl shadow-sm transition-colors"
        >
          <span>Successor Missions</span>
          <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
        </Link>
      </div>

      {loading || !data ? (
        <div className="text-center py-20 text-slate-400 text-sm">
          <div className="w-6 h-6 border-2 border-emerald-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto mb-2" />
          Calculating transparent readiness score...
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          {/* Top Banner: Score Display & Critical Blockers Alert */}
          <div className={`p-6 rounded-2xl border shadow-sm ${
            data.readiness_grade === "Blocked"
              ? "bg-gradient-to-r from-rose-950/20 via-slate-900 to-slate-950 border-rose-500/40 text-white"
              : data.overall_score >= 80
              ? "bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-950/20 via-slate-900 to-slate-950 border-emerald-500/40 text-white"
              : "bg-slate-900 border-slate-800 text-white"
          }`}>
            <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-6">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-6">
                {/* Circular / Large Score Badge */}
                <div className="w-24 h-24 rounded-2xl bg-slate-800/80 border border-slate-700/80 flex flex-col items-center justify-center shadow-inner flex-shrink-0">
                  <span className="text-3xl font-extrabold tracking-tight text-white">
                    {data.overall_score}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 uppercase font-semibold">out of 100</span>
                </div>

                <div className="space-y-1.5">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className={`text-xs font-bold uppercase px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full border ${
                      data.readiness_grade === "Ready"
                        ? "bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-500/40"
                        : data.readiness_grade === "High Confidence"
                        ? "bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-300 border-blue-500/40"
                        : data.readiness_grade === "Blocked"
                        ? "bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 border-rose-500/40"
                        : "bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border-amber-500/40"
                    }`}>
                      Status: {data.readiness_grade}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                      • {data.is_deployable ? "Deployable to Incoming Team" : "Action Required Before Sign-off"}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">
                    {data.readiness_grade === "Blocked" 
                      ? "Handover Blocked: Unresolved Critical Threats Detected" 
                      : data.overall_score >= 80
                      ? "High-Confidence Knowledge Handover Verified"
                      : "Handover In Progress: Key Gaps Pending"}
                  </h2>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 max-w-xl leading-relaxed">
                    {data.readiness_grade === "Blocked"
                      ? "A score must never imply false certainty. Even with moderate numerical score, active contradictions or unassigned root credentials prevent safe handover sign-off."
                      : "All 6 weighted pillars are dynamically evaluated against database claims, living runbooks, and source chunks."}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
