FILE 103: frontend/src/app/login/page.tsx
ABSOLUTE PATH: C:\Users\Omkar\Downloads\Baton-main\frontend\src\app\login\page.tsx
####################################################################################################

"use client";

import React, { useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import Link from "next/link";
import { api } from "@/lib/api";
import { UserRole } from "@/lib/types";
import { ShieldCheck, Lock, Mail, User, ArrowRight, Shield, AlertCircle, CheckCircle2 } from "lucide-react";

type AuthTab = "signin" | "register" | "forgot";

export default function LoginPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<AuthTab>("signin");

  // Sign In fields
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("admin@baton.local");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("AdminPass123!");

  // Register fields
  const [regName, setRegName] = useState("");
  const [regEmail, setRegEmail] = useState("");
  const [regPassword, setRegPassword] = useState("");
  const [regRole, setRegRole] = useState<UserRole>("successor");

  // Forgot password
  const [forgotEmail, setForgotEmail] = useState("");
  const [forgotSuccess, setForgotSuccess] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [successMsg, setSuccessMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleLogin = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    setSuccessMsg(null);
    try {
      await api.login(email, password);
      router.push("/dashboard");
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message || "Invalid email or password");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleRegister = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    setSuccessMsg(null);
    try {
      await api.register(regName, regEmail, regPassword, regRole);
      setSuccessMsg("Account created successfully! You can now sign in.");
      setTab("signin");
      setEmail(regEmail);
      setPassword(regPassword);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message || "Registration failed");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleForgotPassword = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const res = await api.forgotPassword(forgotEmail);
      setForgotSuccess(res.message || "If the email exists, a password reset link has been dispatched.");
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message || "Request failed");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleQuickLogin = async (role: UserRole) => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await api.switchRole(role);
      router.push("/dashboard");
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError("Quick login failed: " + err.message);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 flex flex-col justify-center py-12 px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
      <div className="sm:mx-auto sm:w-full sm:max-w-md text-center">
        <Link href="/" className="inline-flex items-center gap-2.5 mb-3">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-brand-navy flex items-center justify-center text-white shadow-xs">
            <ShieldCheck className="w-6 h-6 text-brand-blue" />
          </div>
          <span className="font-extrabold text-2xl text-slate-900 tracking-tight">BATON</span>
        </Link>
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">
          Project Continuity Intelligence
        </h2>
        <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">
          Pass the project, not just the files. Evidence-linked handover platform.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6 sm:mx-auto sm:w-full sm:max-w-md">
        <div className="bg-white py-6 px-6 shadow-sm rounded-xl sm:px-8 border border-slate-200">
          {/* Navigation Tabs */}
          <div className="flex border-b border-slate-200 mb-5">
            <button
              onClick={() => { setTab("signin"); setError(null); }}
              className={`flex-1 pb-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-center border-b-2 transition-colors ${
                tab === "signin"
                  ? "border-brand-blue text-brand-blue"
                  : "border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800"
              }`}
            >
              Sign In
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => { setTab("register"); setError(null); }}
              className={`flex-1 pb-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-center border-b-2 transition-colors ${
                tab === "register"
                  ? "border-brand-blue text-brand-blue"
                  : "border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800"
              }`}
            >
              Register
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => { setTab("forgot"); setError(null); }}
              className={`flex-1 pb-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-center border-b-2 transition-colors ${
                tab === "forgot"
                  ? "border-brand-blue text-brand-blue"
                  : "border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800"
              }`}
            >
              Reset Key
            </button>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <div className="mb-4 p-3 rounded-lg bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 text-xs text-rose-700 flex items-start gap-2">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
              <span>{error}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {successMsg && (
            <div className="mb-4 p-3 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-xs text-emerald-700 flex items-start gap-2">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
              <span>{successMsg}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Form: Sign In */}
          {tab === "signin" && (
            <form onSubmit={handleLogin} className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Email Address</label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <Mail className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3 top-2.5" />
                  <input
                    type="email"
                    required
                    value={email}
                    onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-blue/20"
                    placeholder="you@company.com"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
                  <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700">Password</label>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setTab("forgot")}
                    className="text-[11px] text-brand-blue hover:underline"
                  >
                    Forgot password?
                  </button>
                </div>
                <div className="relative">
                  <Lock className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3 top-2.5" />
                  <input
                    type="password"
                    required
                    value={password}
                    onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-blue/20"
                    placeholder="Enter your password"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loading}
                className="w-full py-2 bg-brand-blue hover:bg-brand-dark text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold shadow-xs transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
              >
                {loading ? "Authenticating..." : "Sign In with Credentials"}
              </button>
            </form>
          )}

          {/* Form: Register */}
          {tab === "register" && (
            <form onSubmit={handleRegister} className="space-y-3.5">
              <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Full Name</label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <User className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3 top-2.5" />
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={regName}
                    onChange={(e) => setRegName(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-blue/20"
                    placeholder="Jane Doe"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Email Address</label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <Mail className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3 top-2.5" />
                  <input
                    type="email"
                    required
                    value={regEmail}
                    onChange={(e) => setRegEmail(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-blue/20"
                    placeholder="jane@company.com"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Password (8+ chars)</label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <Lock className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3 top-2.5" />
                  <input
                    type="password"
                    required
                    minLength={8}
                    value={regPassword}
                    onChange={(e) => setRegPassword(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-blue/20"
                    placeholder="••••••••"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Assigned Role</label>
                <select
                  value={regRole}
                  onChange={(e) => setRegRole(e.target.value as UserRole)}
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-blue/20 bg-white"
                >
                  <option value="successor">Successor (Receives Handover)</option>
                  <option value="leaver">Leaver (Project Owner Exiting)</option>
                  <option value="reviewer">Reviewer (Audits & Verifies Claims)</option>
                  <option value="restricted_viewer">Restricted Viewer (Public Only)</option>
                </select>
                <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-1 block">
                  Admin roles are strictly provisioned by existing Organization Administrators.
                </span>
              </div>

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loading}
                className="w-full py-2 bg-brand-blue hover:bg-brand-dark text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold shadow-xs transition-colors"
              >
                {loading ? "Registering Account..." : "Create Account"}
              </button>
            </form>
          )}

          {/* Form: Forgot Password */}
          {tab === "forgot" && (
            <form onSubmit={handleForgotPassword} className="space-y-4">
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                Enter your verified email address to receive a cryptographically signed, single-use password reset token.
              </p>

              <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Email Address</label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <Mail className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3 top-2.5" />
                  <input
                    type="email"
                    required
                    value={forgotEmail}
                    onChange={(e) => setForgotEmail(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-blue/20"
                    placeholder="you@company.com"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              {forgotSuccess && (
                <div className="p-3 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 rounded-lg text-xs text-emerald-800">
                  {forgotSuccess}
                </div>
              )}

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loading}
                className="w-full py-2 bg-brand-blue hover:bg-brand-dark text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold shadow-xs transition-colors"
              >

          {/* Quick Demo Switcher for Evaluation */}
          <div className="mt-6 pt-5 border-t border-slate-100 space-y-3">

              <button
                type="button"

              >
                <div className="font-bold text-purple-900">Admin</div>
                <div className="text-[10px] text-purple-700">Sarah Chen</div>
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleQuickLogin("leaver")}
                className="p-2 border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50/50 hover:bg-amber-100 rounded-lg text-left text-xs transition-colors"
              >
                <div className="font-bold text-amber-900">Leaver</div>
                <div className="text-[10px] text-amber-700">Alex Mercer</div>
              </button>

                type="button"

                className="p-2 border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50/50 hover:bg-blue-100 rounded-lg text-left text-xs transition-colors"
              >

}

import Link from "next/link";
import { 
  ShieldCheck, 
  ArrowRight, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  Layers, 
  Lock, 
  SearchCode, 
  Award, 
  FileStack, 
  Bot, 
  HelpCircle,
  AlertTriangle,
  FileSpreadsheet,
  Clock,
  Sparkles
} from "lucide-react";

export default function LandingPage() {
  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-950 text-white selection:bg-brand-blue selection:text-white">
      {/* Navigation */}
      <header className="border-b border-slate-800/80 bg-slate-950/70 backdrop-blur-md sticky top-0 z-50">

          Pass the project, <br />

            not just the files.
          </span>
        </h1>

          is grounded in verifiable citations.

####################################################################################################

"use client";

import React, { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { api } from "@/lib/api";
import { UserRole } from "@/lib/types";
import { 
  UserCheck, 
  RotateCcw, 
  LogOut, 
  Shield, 
  ChevronDown, 
  Check, 
  Sparkles,
  Bot
} from "lucide-react";

const ROLES: { id: UserRole; label: string; name: string; color: string }[] = [
  { id: "admin", label: "Admin", name: "Sarah Chen", color: "bg-purple-100 text-purple-800 border-purple-200" },
  { id: "leaver", label: "Leaver", name: "Alex Mercer", color: "bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border-amber-200" },
  { id: "reviewer", label: "Reviewer", name: "Elena Rostova", color: "bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 border-blue-200" },
  { id: "successor", label: "Successor", name: "Sam Taylor", color: "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-200" },
  { id: "restricted_viewer", label: "Restricted Viewer", name: "Jordan Lee", color: "bg-slate-100 text-slate-800 border-slate-200" },
];

export function Header() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [currentUser, setCurrentUser] = useState<any>(null);
  const [roleDropdownOpen, setRoleDropdownOpen] = useState(false);
  const [resetting, setResetting] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const user = api.getStoredUser();
    if (user) {
      setCurrentUser(user);
    } else {
      // Auto login as admin for initial exploration if unauthenticated
      api.switchRole("admin").then((u) => setCurrentUser(u));
    }
  }, []);

  const handleRoleChange = async (targetRole: UserRole) => {
    try {
      const u = await api.switchRole(targetRole);
      setCurrentUser(u);
      setRoleDropdownOpen(false);
      window.location.reload();
    } catch (err) {
      alert("Failed to switch role");
    }
  };

  const handleResetDemo = async () => {
    if (confirm("Reset Project Aether demonstration dataset to clean original state?")) {
      setResetting(true);
      try {
        await api.resetDemo();
        alert("Project Aether dataset successfully reset!");
        window.location.reload();
      } catch (err: any) {
        alert("Reset failed: " + err.message);
      } finally {
        setResetting(false);
      }
    }
  };

  const handleLogout = () => {
    api.logout();
    router.push("/login");
  };

  const currentRoleInfo = ROLES.find((r) => r.id === currentUser?.role) || ROLES[0];

  return (
    <header className="h-16 bg-white border-b border-slate-200 px-6 flex items-center justify-between sticky top-0 z-30 shadow-sm">
      {/* Left: Project Selector */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-brand-blue" />
          <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase text-slate-400 tracking-wider">Active Workspace</span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg">
          <Bot className="w-4 h-4 text-brand-blue" />
          <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">Project Aether (Robotics AMR)</span>
          <span className="text-[10px] font-bold bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded border border-amber-200">
            SYNTHETIC DEMO
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Right: Role Switcher & Controls */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        {/* Reset Demo Button */}
        <button
          onClick={handleResetDemo}
          disabled={resetting}
          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg transition-colors"
          title="Reset Project Aether to initial synthetic state"
        >
          <RotateCcw className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${resetting ? "animate-spin" : ""}`} />
          <span>{resetting ? "Resetting..." : "Reset Demo"}</span>
        </button>

        {/* Role Switcher Dropdown */}
        <div className="relative">
          <button
            onClick={() => setRoleDropdownOpen(!roleDropdownOpen)}
            className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 transition-colors shadow-xs"
          >
            <Shield className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-brand-blue" />
            <div className="text-left">
              <div className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">Current View Role</div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800">{currentRoleInfo.name}</span>
                <span className={`text-[10px] font-semibold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded border ${currentRoleInfo.color}`}>
                  {currentRoleInfo.label}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <ChevronDown className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400 ml-1" />
          </button>

          {roleDropdownOpen && (
            <div className="absolute right-0 mt-2 w-72 bg-white rounded-xl shadow-xl border border-slate-200 py-2 z-50 animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-top-2">
              <div className="px-3 py-2 border-b border-slate-100">
                <div className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800">Simulate User Perspective</div>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">
                  Switch roles to verify permission partitions & canary shielding in real time.
                </p>
              </div>
              <div className="p-1 space-y-1">
                {ROLES.map((r) => {
                  const isSelected = r.id === currentUser?.role;
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={r.id}
                      onClick={() => handleRoleChange(r.id)}
                      className={`w-full flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2 text-left rounded-lg text-xs transition-colors ${
                        isSelected ? "bg-blue-50 font-semibold text-brand-blue" : "hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700"
                      }`}
                    >
                      <div>
                        <div className="font-medium text-slate-900">{r.name}</div>
                        <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">{r.label} Clearance</div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span className={`text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded border ${r.color}`}>
                          {r.label}
                        </span>
                        {isSelected && <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-brand-blue" />}
                      </div>
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* User profile & logout */}
        <button
          onClick={handleLogout}
          className="p-2 text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-50 rounded-lg transition-colors"
          title="Sign Out"
        >
          <LogOut className="w-4 h-4" />
        </button>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

import React from "react";
import { SensitivityLevel } from "@/lib/types";
import { Globe, Lock, ShieldAlert, KeyRound } from "lucide-react";

export function SensitivityBadge({ level, sensitivity }: { level?: SensitivityLevel; sensitivity?: SensitivityLevel }) {
  const effectiveLevel = level || sensitivity;
  switch (effectiveLevel) {
    case "public":
      return (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs font-medium bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200">
          <Globe className="w-3 h-3 text-slate-500" />
          Public
        </span>
      );
    case "internal":
      return (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs font-medium bg-blue-50 text-blue-800 border border-blue-200">
          <Lock className="w-3 h-3 text-blue-500" />
          Internal
        </span>
      );
    case "confidential":
      return (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs font-medium bg-amber-50 text-amber-800 border border-amber-200">
          <KeyRound className="w-3 h-3 text-amber-500" />
          Confidential
        </span>
      );
    case "restricted":
      return (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs font-medium bg-red-50 text-red-800 border border-red-200">
          <ShieldAlert className="w-3 h-3 text-red-600" />
          Restricted / Canary
        </span>
      );
    default:
      return null;
  }
}

"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { api } from "@/lib/api";
import { UserRole } from "@/lib/types";
import {
  LayoutDashboard,
  FolderGit2,
  Layers,
  FileSpreadsheet,
  ShieldCheck,
  ChevronRight,
  LogOut,
  User as UserIcon,
  RefreshCw
} from "lucide-react";

const PRIMARY_MODULES = [
  { 
    name: "Overview", 
    href: "/dashboard", 
    icon: LayoutDashboard,
    description: "Summary, risks & knowledge coverage"
  },
  { 
    name: "Projects", 
    href: "/dashboard/projects", 
    icon: FolderGit2,
    description: "Sources, timeline & knowledge graph"
  },
  { 
    name: "Knowledge Ledger", 
    href: "/dashboard/ledger", 
    icon: Layers,
    description: "Claims, evidence & verification pipeline"
  },
  { 
    name: "Handover Center", 
    href: "/dashboard/handovers", 
    icon: FileSpreadsheet,
    description: "Review, gap interview & missions"
  },
  { 
    name: "Trust & Security", 
    href: "/dashboard/security", 
    icon: ShieldCheck,
    description: "RBAC, Presidio privacy & test runner"
  },
];

export function Sidebar() {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [user, setUser] = useState<{ full_name: string; email: string; role: UserRole } | null>(null);
  const [switching, setSwitching] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const stored = api.getStoredUser();
    if (stored) {
      setUser(stored);
    } else {
      api.getMe().then(setUser).catch(() => null);
    }
  }, [pathname]);

  const handleRoleSwitch = async (role: UserRole) => {
    setSwitching(true);
    try {
      const updated = await api.switchRole(role);
      setUser(updated);
      window.location.reload();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Failed to switch role:", err);
    } finally {
      setSwitching(false);
    }
  };

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    await api.logout();
    router.push("/login");
  };

  return (
    <aside className="w-64 bg-slate-950 text-slate-200 border-r border-slate-800 flex flex-col flex-shrink-0 min-h-screen select-none">
      {/* Brand Header */}
      <div className="h-16 flex items-center px-6 border-b border-slate-800 gap-3 bg-slate-900/50">
        <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-blue-600 flex items-center justify-center text-white shadow-sm font-bold text-sm">
          B
        </div>
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="font-bold text-base text-white tracking-tight">BATON</span>
            <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-400 bg-slate-800 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded border border-slate-700">
              Enterprise
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 font-normal">Knowledge Continuity</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 5 Primary Modules Navigation */}
      <div className="flex-1 py-6 px-3 space-y-1">
        <div className="px-3 pb-2 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500">
          Core Platform
        </div>
        
        {PRIMARY_MODULES.map((item) => {
          const isOverview = item.href === "/dashboard";
          const isActive = isOverview 
            ? pathname === "/dashboard"
            : pathname.startsWith(item.href) || (item.name === "Knowledge Ledger" && pathname.startsWith("/dashboard/claims"));
          
          const Icon = item.icon;

          return (
            <Link
              key={item.href}
              href={item.href}
              className={`flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${
                isActive
                  ? "bg-blue-600 text-white font-semibold shadow-xs"
                  : "text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-900 hover:text-white"
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <Icon className={`w-4 h-4 ${isActive ? "text-white" : "text-slate-400"}`} />
                <div>
                  <span className="block text-xs leading-none">{item.name}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              {isActive && <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-blue-200" />}
            </Link>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {/* User Context & Role Switcher */}
      <div className="p-3 border-t border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/40 space-y-2.5">
        <div className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 space-y-2">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 truncate pr-2">
              <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-200 flex-shrink-0">
                {user?.full_name ? user.full_name.charAt(0) : "U"}
              </div>
              <div className="truncate">
                <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-200 block truncate leading-tight">
                  {user?.full_name || "Active Member"}
                </span>
                <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-mono block truncate">
                  {user?.email || "user@baton.local"}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              onClick={handleLogout}
              title="Sign Out"
              className="p-1 hover:bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-400 rounded transition-colors flex-shrink-0"
            >
              <LogOut className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Quick Active Role Selector for Testing */}
          <div className="pt-1.5 border-t border-slate-800/80 flex items-center justify-between gap-1.5 text-[10px]">
            <span className="text-slate-400 font-medium">Role:</span>
            <select
              value={user?.role || "successor"}
              onChange={(e) => handleRoleSwitch(e.target.value as UserRole)}
              disabled={switching}
              className="bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-slate-200 text-[10px] font-semibold rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 focus:outline-none capitalize cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              <option value="admin">Admin</option>
              <option value="reviewer">Reviewer</option>
              <option value="leaver">Leaver</option>
              <option value="successor">Successor</option>
              <option value="restricted_viewer">Restricted Viewer</option>
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
}

import React from "react";
import { ClaimStatus } from "@/lib/types";
import { ShieldCheck, FileCheck, HelpCircle, AlertTriangle, HelpCircle as MissingIcon, AlertCircle } from "lucide-react";

export function StatusBadge({ status }: { status: ClaimStatus }) {
  switch (status) {
    case "Verified":
      return (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200">
          <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" />
          Verified
        </span>
      );
    case "Evidence-backed":
      return (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200">
          <FileCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-blue-600" />
          Evidence-backed
        </span>
      );
    case "Inferred":
      return (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border border-amber-200">
          <HelpCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-600" />
          Inferred
        </span>
      );
    case "Conflict":
      return (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border border-rose-200">
          <AlertTriangle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-600" />
          Conflict
        </span>
      );
    case "Missing":
      return (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 border border-purple-200">
          <MissingIcon className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-purple-600" />
          Missing Gap
        </span>
      );
    case "Unverified":
      return (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-300">
          <AlertCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-500" />
          Unverified
        </span>
      );
    default:
      return (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs bg-slate-100 text-slate-700">
          {status}
        </span>
      );
  }
}

import { UserRole } from "./types";

const API_BASE_URL = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL || "http://127.0.0.1:8000/api/v1";

class ApiClient {
  private getAuthHeaders(): HeadersInit {
    const headers: HeadersInit = {
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
    };
    if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
      const token = localStorage.getItem("baton_token");
      if (token) {
        headers["Authorization"] = `Bearer ${token}`;
      }
    }
    return headers;
  }

  async request<T>(endpoint: string, options: RequestInit = {}): Promise<T> {
    const url = `${API_BASE_URL}${endpoint}`;
    const defaultHeaders = options.body instanceof FormData ? {} : this.getAuthHeaders();
    
    // Merge headers if provided
    const headers = {
      ...defaultHeaders,
      ...(options.headers || {}),
    };

    if (options.body instanceof FormData && typeof window !== "undefined") {
      const token = localStorage.getItem("baton_token");
      if (token) {
        (headers as Record<string, string>)["Authorization"] = `Bearer ${token}`;
      }
    }

    const response = await fetch(url, {
      ...options,
      headers,
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      let errorDetail = "API request failed";
      try {
        const errJson = await response.json();
        errorDetail = errJson.detail || JSON.stringify(errJson);
      } catch {
        errorDetail = await response.text();
      }
      throw new Error(errorDetail);
    }

    return response.json();
  }

  async register(fullName: string, email: string, password: string, role?: UserRole) {
    return this.request<any>("/auth/register", {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({ full_name: fullName, email, password, role }),
    });
  }

  async forgotPassword(email: string) {
    return this.request<any>("/auth/forgot-password", {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({ email }),
    });
  }

  async resetPassword(token: string, newPassword: string) {
    return this.request<any>("/auth/reset-password", {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({ token, new_password: newPassword }),
    });
  }

  async verifyEmail(token: string) {
    return this.request<any>("/auth/verify-email", {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({ token }),
    });
  }

  async logout() {
    try {
      await this.request<any>("/auth/logout", { method: "POST" });
    } catch {
      // Ignore network errors on logout
    }
    if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
      localStorage.removeItem("baton_token");
      localStorage.removeItem("baton_user");
    }
  }

  async revokeAllSessions() {
    return this.request<any>("/auth/revoke-all-sessions", { method: "POST" });
  }

  async updateProfile(fullName: string) {
    return this.request<any>("/auth/profile", {
      method: "PATCH",
      body: JSON.stringify({ full_name: fullName }),
    });
  }

  async getMe() {
    return this.request<any>("/auth/me");
  }

  async getUsersList() {
    return this.request<any[]>("/auth/users");
  }

  async changeUserRole(userId: string, newRole: string) {
    return this.request<any>(`/auth/users/${userId}/role`, {
      method: "PATCH",
      body: JSON.stringify({ new_role: newRole }),
    });
  }

  // Auth Endpoints
  async login(email: string, password: string) {
    const data = await this.request<{
      access_token: string;
      user_id: string;
      email: string;
      full_name: string;
      role: UserRole;
    }>("/auth/login", {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }),
    });
    if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
      localStorage.setItem("baton_token", data.access_token);
      localStorage.setItem("baton_user", JSON.stringify(data));
    }
    return data;
  }

  async switchRole(targetRole: UserRole) {
    const data = await this.request<{
      access_token: string;
      user_id: string;
      email: string;
      full_name: string;
      role: UserRole;
    }>(`/auth/switch-demo-role?target_role=${targetRole}`, {
      method: "POST",
    });
    if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
      localStorage.setItem("baton_token", data.access_token);
      localStorage.setItem("baton_user", JSON.stringify(data));
    }
    return data;
  }

  getStoredUser() {
    if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
      const userStr = localStorage.getItem("baton_user");
      if (userStr) {
        try {
          return JSON.parse(userStr);
        } catch {
          return null;
        }
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  // Analytics
  async getAnalytics() {
    return this.request<any>("/analytics/dashboard");
  }

  // Projects
  async getProjects() {
    return this.request<any[]>("/projects");
  }

  async getProject(id: string) {
    return this.request<any>(`/projects/${id}`);
  }

  async createProject(name: string, description?: string, repositoryUrl?: string) {
    return this.request<any>("/projects", {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({ name, description, repository_url: repositoryUrl }),
    });
  }

  // Sources
  async getSources(projectId: string) {
    return this.request<any[]>(`/sources/project/${projectId}`);
  }

  async uploadSource(formData: FormData) {
    return this.request<any>("/sources/upload", {
      method: "POST",
      body: formData,
    });
  }

  // Claims
  async getClaims(projectId: string, params: Record<string, string> = {}) {
    const query = new URLSearchParams(params).toString();
    const endpoint = `/claims/project/${projectId}${query ? `?${query}` : ""}`;
    return this.request<any[]>(endpoint);
  }

  async getClaim(claimId: string) {
    return this.request<any>(`/claims/${claimId}`);
  }

  // Evidence
  async getEvidenceInspection(claimId: string) {
    return this.request<any>(`/evidence/claim/${claimId}`);
  }

  // Gap Interview
  async getGapQuestions(projectId: string) {
    return this.request<any[]>(`/gap-interview/project/${projectId}`);
  }

  async answerGapQuestion(questionId: string, answerText: string) {
    return this.request<any>(`/gap-interview/questions/${questionId}/answer`, {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({ answer_text: answerText }),
    });
  }

  async skipGapQuestion(questionId: string) {
    return this.request<any>(`/gap-interview/questions/${questionId}/skip`, {
      method: "POST",
    });
  }

  async markUnknownGapQuestion(questionId: string) {
    return this.request<any>(`/gap-interview/questions/${questionId}/mark-unknown`, {
      method: "POST",
    });
  }

  // Review
  async reviewClaim(claimId: string, action: string, notes?: string, editedDescription?: string) {
    return this.request<any>(`/review/claim/${claimId}`, {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({
        action,
        reviewer_notes: notes,
        edited_description: editedDescription,
      }),
    });
  }

  async approveHandover(handoverId: string) {
    return this.request<any>(`/review/handover/${handoverId}/approve`, {
      method: "POST",
    });
  }

  // Handovers
  async getHandovers(projectId: string) {
    return this.request<any[]>(`/handovers/project/${projectId}`);
  }

  async getHandoverRoleView(handoverId: string, targetRole?: string) {
    const endpoint = `/handovers/${handoverId}/role-view${targetRole ? `?target_role=${targetRole}` : ""}`;
    return this.request<any>(endpoint);
  }

  async compileHandover(projectId: string, title?: string) {
    return this.request<any>(`/handovers/compile?project_id=${projectId}&title=${encodeURIComponent(title || "New Version")}`, {
      method: "POST",
    });
  }

  async publishHandover(handoverId: string) {
    return this.request<any>(`/handovers/${handoverId}/publish`, {
      method: "POST",
    });
  }

  async acknowledgeHandover(handoverId: string, quizAnswers: number[], comments?: string) {
    return this.request<any>(`/handovers/${handoverId}/acknowledge`, {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({
        quiz_answers: quizAnswers,
        successor_comments: comments,
      }),
    });
  }

  // Timeline
  async getTimeline(projectId: string) {
    return this.request<any[]>(`/timeline/project/${projectId}`);
  }

  // Audit
  async getAuditLogs(projectId: string) {
    return this.request<any[]>(`/audit/project/${projectId}`);
  }

  // BATON 2.0 Continuity Intelligence API
  async getReadiness(projectId: string) {
    return this.request<any>(`/projects/${projectId}/readiness`);
  }

  async getRiskRadar(projectId: string) {
    return this.request<any>(`/projects/${projectId}/risks`);
  }

  async updateRiskStatus(projectId: string, riskId: string, status: string) {
    return this.request<any>(`/projects/${projectId}/risks/${riskId}?status=${encodeURIComponent(status)}`, {
      method: "PATCH",
    });
  }

  async getKnowledgeGraph(projectId: string) {
    return this.request<any>(`/projects/${projectId}/graph`);
  }

  async getDecisions(projectId: string) {
    return this.request<any[]>(`/projects/${projectId}/decisions`);

    return this.request<any>(`/projects/${projectId}/decisions/${decisionId}`);

    });

  async getMissions(projectId: string) {

  async getRunbooks(projectId: string) {

  async getComments(projectId: string, claimId?: string, runbookId?: string) {
    let url = `/projects/${projectId}/collaboration/comments`;
    const params = [];
    if (claimId) params.push(`claim_id=${claimId}`);
    if (runbookId) params.push(`runbook_id=${runbookId}`);
    if (params.length > 0) url += `?${params.join("&")}`;
    return this.request<any[]>(url);
  }

  async postComment(projectId: string, payload: { claim_id?: string; runbook_id?: string; comment_text: string; status_tag?: string }) {
    return this.request<any>(`/projects/${projectId}/collaboration/comments`, {

export type UserRole = "admin" | "leaver" | "reviewer" | "successor" | "restricted_viewer";
export type SensitivityLevel = "public" | "internal" | "confidential" | "restricted";
export type ClaimStatus = "Verified" | "Evidence-backed" | "Inferred" | "Conflict" | "Missing" | "Unverified";
export type ClaimCategory = 
  | "Decision"
  | "Decision Reasoning"
  | "Task Ownership"
  | "Pending Task"
  | "Dependency"
  | "Blocker"
  | "Risk"
  | "Recurring Problem"
  | "Important Contact"
  | "Deployment & Access"
  | "Timeline Milestone"
  | "Open Question";

export type ReviewState = "pending" | "accepted" | "edited" | "rejected";
export type HandoverStatus = "draft" | "under_review" | "approved" | "published" | "archived";
export type Timeframe = "Day 1" | "Week 1" | "Day 30";

export interface User {
  id: string;
  email: string;
  full_name: string;
  role: UserRole;
  created_at: string;
}

export interface Project {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  slug: string;
  description?: string;
  repository_url?: string;
  created_by: string;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
  total_sources: number;
  total_claims: number;
  total_handovers: number;
}

export interface Source {
  id: string;
  project_id: string;
  filename: string;
  file_type: "repo" | "markdown" | "pdf" | "csv" | "json" | "chat";
  sensitivity: SensitivityLevel;
  consent_confirmed: boolean;
  sha256_hash?: string;
  source_metadata: Record<string, any>;
  created_at: string;
  total_chunks: number;
}

export interface EvidenceSpan {
  id: string;
  chunk_id: string;
  quote: string;
  char_start?: number;
  char_end?: number;
  verified_deterministic: boolean;
  semantic_support_score: number;
}

export interface Claim {
  id: string;
  project_id: string;
  category: ClaimCategory;
  description: string;
  reasoning?: string;
  exact_quote?: string;
  source_id?: string;
  chunk_id?: string;
  evidence_location?: string;
  confidence: number;
  status: ClaimStatus;
  sensitivity: SensitivityLevel;
  review_state: ReviewState;
  reviewer_notes?: string;
  conflict_claim_id?: string;
  event_timestamp?: string;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
  evidence_spans: EvidenceSpan[];
}

export interface GapQuestion {
  id: string;
  project_id: string;
  question_text: string;

  created_at: string;
  answers: {
    id: string;
    question_id: string;
    answered_by: string;
    answer_text: string;
    created_at: string;
  }[];
}

export interface ContinuationAction {
  id: string;
  timeframe: Timeframe;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  assigned_to?: string;
  linked_claim_id?: string;
  priority: string;
  is_verified: boolean;
}

export interface ComprehensionCheck {
  id: string;
  question: string;
  options: string[];
  correct_option_index: number;
  explanation?: string;
}

export interface HandoverVersion {
  id: string;
  project_id: string;
  version_number: number;
  title: string;
  executive_summary: string;
  status: HandoverStatus;
  approved_by?: string;
  approved_at?: string;

  share_expires_at?: string;
  created_at: string;
  actions: ContinuationAction[];
  comprehension_checks: ComprehensionCheck[];
}

export interface AuditLog {
  id: string;
  project_id?: string;
  user_id?: string;
  user_email: string;
  user_name: string;

  created_at: string;
}

export interface AnalyticsData {

# BATON 2.0 – Project Continuity Intelligence Platform
> **Tagline:** *Pass the project, not just the files.*

BATON 2.0 is a production-grade, permission-aware, evidence-linked **Project Continuity Intelligence Platform** designed to prevent critical organizational, engineering, architectural, and operational knowledge from leaving when team members exit.

Unlike conventional document summarizers that hallucinate facts and flatten nuances, BATON 2.0 reconstructs project knowledge into independent, verifiable claims where **every single claim is grounded in verbatim citations from authorized sources**.

---

## 1. System Architecture

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    subgraph Sources ["1. Source Ingestion Layer"]
        A1[GitHub Repository Export] --> P[Ingestion Engine]
        A2[Meeting Notes PDF/MD] --> P
        A3[Task Board CSV/JSON] --> P
        A4[Exported Chat Transcripts] --> P
        P --> Chunk[Deterministic Chunker]
        Chunk --> Privacy[Microsoft Presidio PII & Canary Scanner]
    end

    subgraph CoreEngine ["2. AI Continuity Intelligence Engine"]
        Privacy --> RedactedChunks[Redacted Source Chunks]
        RedactedChunks --> LLM[Swappable LLM Adapter: Gemini / Claude / Deterministic]
        LLM --> Extraction[Decisions, Ownership, PRs, Blockers, Risks, Timeline]
        Extraction --> Verifier[Deterministic Quotation Verifier & Support Checker]
        Verifier --> Ledger[(PostgreSQL 16: Claim Ledger)]
        Ledger --> GraphEngine[Interactive Knowledge Graph Engine]
        Ledger --> Archaeology[Decision Archaeology Engine]
        Ledger --> RadarEngine[8-Category Project Risk Radar]
        Ledger --> ReadinessEngine[0-100 Weighted Handover Readiness Engine]
        Ledger --> DriftEngine[Temporal Knowledge Drift Detector]
        Ledger --> BusFactorEngine[Bus-Factor & Single-Person Silo Detector]
    end

    subgraph Defense ["3. Zero-Hallucination Assistant & Active Defense Lab"]
        RedactedChunks --> AssistantRAG[Ask Your Project: Permission-Aware RAG]
        AssistantRAG --> GroundedResp[Grounded Citations & Uncertainty Labels]
        AssistantRAG --> CanaryShield[Canary Secret Shield & Leak Refusal]
        CanaryShield --> SecurityLab[Canary Penetration Test Simulator]
    end

    subgraph Onboarding ["4. Successor Experience & Governance"]
        ReadinessEngine --> Missions[Guided Successor Mission Mode: 6 Steps]
        Ledger --> LivingRunbooks[Living Runbooks: Setup, Deployment, Disaster Recovery]
        Ledger --> ReviewWorkspace[Human Review Workspace]
        ReviewWorkspace --> CollabRoom[Multi-Role Collaboration Room]
        ReviewWorkspace --> HandoverCompiler[Role-Partitioned Handover Compiler]
        HandoverCompiler --> Quiz[Comprehension Quiz & SHA-256 Receipt Hash]
    end
```

---

## 2. BATON 2.0 Core Modules & Capabilities

| Module | Purpose | Live Route |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Project Knowledge Graph** | Interactive SVG topology mapping team members, decisions, tasks, systems, files, and dependencies with zoom/pan and evidence drawer. | `/dashboard/graph` |
| **Decision Archaeology** | Reconstructs what was decided, participants, alternatives considered, concerns raised, and reversal status; missing reasoning is explicitly flagged. | `/dashboard/decisions` |
| **Project Risk Radar** | 8-dimension risk heatmap (knowledge concentration, ownership, deadline, dependency, docs, security, debt, unresolved decisions) with mitigations. | `/dashboard/risks` |
| **Handover Readiness Score** | Deterministic 0–100 score across 6 weighted pillars with transparent point deduction breakdowns and concrete remediation checklist. | `/dashboard/readiness` |
| **Ask Your Project AI** | Permission-filtered conversational assistant for incoming successors grounded strictly in verbatim citations; refuses canary leaks. | `/dashboard/assistant` |
| **Successor Mission Mode** | 6 interactive onboarding missions (Architecture, Dependencies, Local Setup, Simulation, Blocker Resolution, Production Deployment). | `/dashboard/missions` |
| **Living Runbooks** | Dynamic operational runbooks (Setup, Deployment, Troubleshooting, Recurring, Emergency Disaster Recovery) with runnable snippets & reviewer sign-off. | `/dashboard/runbooks` |
| **Bus-Factor Intelligence** | Identifies single-person bottlenecks (Bus Factor = 1), zero-backup components, and urgency handoff action plans. | `/dashboard/bus-factor` |
| **Knowledge Drift Detector** | Temporal divergence analysis comparing baseline sources vs current drift across decisions, scripts, and ownership. | `/dashboard/drift` |
| **Security & Canary Lab** | Automated penetration testing simulator evaluating direct retrieval, paraphrasing, and RAG injection attacks across user roles (100% containment). | `/dashboard/security-lab` |
| **Collaboration Room** | Multi-role deliberation thread connecting outgoing leads, technical reviewers, and incoming successors. | `/dashboard/collaboration` |
| **Claim Ledger & Split Evidence** | Verified independent knowledge claims with side-by-side verbatim citation highlighting. | `/dashboard/claims` & `/dashboard/evidence` |
| **Role-Partitioned Handover** | Successor, Reviewer, and Restricted Viewer views with 30-day continuation plan and cryptographic receipt. | `/dashboard/handovers` |

---

## 3. Handover Readiness Scoring Formula

The transparent 0–100 score is computed from actual database records:

$$\text{Readiness Score} = S_{\text{evidence}} (25\%) + S_{\text{ownership}} (20\%) + S_{\text{conflict}} (15\%) + S_{\text{dependency}} (15\%) + S_{\text{access}} (15\%) + S_{\text{review}} (10\%)$$

> [!IMPORTANT]
> **Safety Rule:** A high numerical score never implies false certainty. Active critical blockers or unresolved contradictions automatically set the project status to **Blocked** or **At Risk**, preventing unapproved sign-off.

## 5. Verification & Testing

### Running Backend Pytest Suite
```powershell
cd backend
python -m pytest tests
```
*Result: 11 passed (Canary Leak Prevention, Citation Verification, Handover Scoring, Knowledge Graph, Assistant RAG, Drift Detector, Security Simulator).*

### Running Production Frontend
```powershell
cd frontend
npm run build
npm run start -- -p 3000
```
Visit `http://localhost:3000` to launch the platform.




####################################################################################################
FILE 114: run_locally - Copy.txt
ABSOLUTE PATH: C:\Users\Omkar\Downloads\Baton-main\run_locally - Copy.txt
####################################################################################################

Write-Host "==========================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "   BATON 2.0 - Project Continuity Intelligence Platform" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "==========================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan

# 1. PostgreSQL Cluster (Port 5435)
$pgConn = Get-NetTCPConnection -LocalPort 5435 -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
if (-not $pgConn) {
    Write-Host "[1/3] Starting PostgreSQL cluster on port 5435..." -ForegroundColor Yellow
    Start-Process -FilePath "C:\Program Files\PostgreSQL\16\bin\postgres.exe" -ArgumentList "-D", "$PSScriptRoot\pg_data", "-p", "5435" -WindowStyle Hidden
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 3
} else {
    Write-Host "[1/3] PostgreSQL is already active on port 5435." -ForegroundColor Green
}

# 2. FastAPI Backend (Port 8000)
$beConn = Get-NetTCPConnection -LocalPort 8000 -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
if (-not $beConn) {
    Write-Host "[2/3] Starting FastAPI Backend on port 8000..." -ForegroundColor Yellow
    Start-Process -FilePath "powershell.exe" -ArgumentList "-NoExit", "-Command", "Set-Location '$PSScriptRoot\backend'; python -m uvicorn app.main:app --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8000"
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 3
} else {
    Write-Host "[2/3] FastAPI Backend is already active on port 8000." -ForegroundColor Green
}

# 3. Next.js Production Frontend (Port 3000)
$feConn = Get-NetTCPConnection -LocalPort 3000 -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
if (-not $feConn) {
    Write-Host "[3/3] Starting Next.js Frontend on port 3000..." -ForegroundColor Yellow
    Start-Process -FilePath "powershell.exe" -ArgumentList "-NoExit", "-Command", "Set-Location '$PSScriptRoot\frontend'; npm run start -- -p 3000"
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 3
} else {
    Write-Host "[3/3] Next.js Frontend is already active on port 3000." -ForegroundColor Green
}

Write-Host "Opening BATON 2.0 in browser: http://localhost:3000 ..." -ForegroundColor Cyan
Start-Process "http://localhost:3000"
